USER # PMS application state transitions (authoritative table, merged from all 16 basis workflows; read-only spec)
USER 
USER | From state | Actor | Use case | Guard / outcome | Resulting state |
USER |---|---|---|---|---|
USER 
USER | Draft / Not Submitted | Applicant | UC-PMS-001 / Submit Patent Application | Submission passes completeness/document validation | Submitted |
USER | Submitted | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-007 / Assign Application to Director | Eligible Director selected or valid automatic routing succeeds | Under Review |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Decision = Approve; justification >=50 characters; review within deadline | Approved |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Decision = Needs Revision; valid justification | Needs Revision |
USER | Needs Revision | Applicant | UC-PMS-005 / Revise and Resubmit Application | Within 60 days; revision addresses feedback | Resubmitted |
USER | Resubmitted | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-007 / Assign Application to Director | Valid assignment/reassignment | Under Review |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Decision = Reject; valid justification | Rejected |
USER | Rejected | Applicant | UC-PMS-022 / File Appeal Against Rejection | Appeal filed within allowed period with grounds/justification | Appeal Submitted |
USER | Appeal Submitted | Director | UC-PMS-023 / Review Appeal | Appeal outcome = Remand for Amendment | Remanded for Amendment |
USER | Appeal Submitted | Director | UC-PMS-023 / Review Appeal | Appeal outcome = Uphold Rejection | Rejection Upheld |
USER | Appeal Submitted | Director | UC-PMS-023 / Review Appeal | Appeal outcome = Modify Decision | Decision Modified |
USER | Approved | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-010 / Assign Attorney to Approved Application | Assignment satisfies panel/expertise/workload/conflict/preference rules | Assignment Offered |
USER | Assignment Offered | Attorney | UC-PMS-011 / Accept or Decline Attorney Assignment | Outcome = Accept | Attorney Assigned |
USER | Assignment Offered | Attorney | UC-PMS-011 / Accept or Decline Attorney Assignment | Outcome = Decline | Pending Review |
USER | Pending Review | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-010 / Assign Attorney to Approved Application | Valid reassignment to another eligible Attorney | Assignment Offered |
USER | Approved | Director | UC-PMS-009 / Review and Decide Patent Budget | Cost within applicable threshold; budget availability validated where data exists; outcome = Approve | Budget Approved |
USER | Approved | Director | UC-PMS-009 / Review and Decide Patent Budget | Outcome = Reject | Budget Rejected |
USER | Approved | Director | UC-PMS-009 / Review and Decide Patent Budget | Total cost exceeds configured threshold | Escalated |
USER | Escalated | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-027 / Monitor Workflow and Handle Exceptional Cases | Handle/route the exceptional case within PMS scope | Escalation Tracked / External Decision Pending |
USER | Pre-Filing Preconditions Satisfied | Attorney | UC-PMS-013 / Prepare and Record External Patent Filing | Required approvals and budget complete; inventor agreements complete; applicable format rules satisfied; international justification if needed | Filed |
USER | Filed / Post-Filing Active | Attorney | UC-PMS-014 / Record Patent Office Communication | Official communication is an office action | Office Action Recorded |
USER | Office Action Recorded | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Applicant review is required; draft prepared | Awaiting Applicant Approval |
USER | Awaiting Applicant Approval | Applicant | UC-PMS-029 / Review Office-Action Response | Applicant records approval | Applicant Approved |
USER | Applicant Approved | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Response finalized and recorded submitted within deadline or permitted extension | Response Submitted |
USER | Office Action Recorded | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Applicant review not required; draft finalized | Response Ready |
USER | Response Ready | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Response recorded submitted within deadline or permitted extension | Response Submitted |
USER | Granted / Maintenance Cycle Active | Attorney | UC-PMS-017 / Manage Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance | Maintenance decision is due | Renewal Decision Requested |
USER | Renewal Decision Requested | Director | UC-PMS-018 / Make Maintenance Renewal Decision | Outcome = Renew | Renewal Approved |
USER | Renewal Approved | Attorney | UC-PMS-017 / Manage Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance | Maintenance action recorded; next cycle scheduled | Renewed / Next Cycle Scheduled |
USER | Renewal Decision Requested | Director | UC-PMS-018 / Make Maintenance Renewal Decision | Outcome = Do Not Renew | Do Not Renew |
USER | Any configured withdrawal-eligible pre-filing state | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing; current status is withdrawal-eligible; reason provided; co-inventor consent if required | Withdrawn |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Needs Revision | BR-PMS-029; BR-PMS-030; BR-PMS-031 |
USER | Renewal Decision Requested | Director | UC-PMS-018 / Make Maintenance Renewal Decision | Do Not Renew | BR-PMS-061 |
USER | Submitted | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Under Review | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Needs Revision | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Rejected | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Approved | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Attorney Assigned | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Pending Review | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER ```

USER core/db/mixins.py
USER ```
USER from django.db import models
USER 
USER 
USER class TimeStampedModel(models.Model):
USER     created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
USER     updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         abstract = True
USER 
USER 
USER class UserScopedModel(models.Model):
USER     """A row belonging to a person.
USER 
USER     `user_id` is the IAM user id. It is a plain integer and NOT a ForeignKey —
USER     there is no user table in this database, and cross-boundary references must
USER     stay unconstrained so a module can be extracted later without schema
USER     surgery.
USER     """
USER 
USER     user_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         abstract = True
USER ```

USER ../elm_specs_md/pms_kit/chunks/problem_statement.md
USER ```
USER # PMS problem statement (read-only spec)
USER 
USER 2.1 Overview
USER The Patent Management System (PMS) digitizes and streamlines institutional patent application management and intellectual property protection within Fusion ERP.
USER The module supports application submission, application documentation, review and decision workflows, revision and resubmission, attorney assignment, legal and patentability assessment, budget approval and financial tracking, external patent-office filing support, post-grant tracking, maintenance fee tracking, office-action response tracking, withdrawal, co-inventor and agreement management, prior-art search support, document version management, patent licensing and technology-transfer request tracking, appeal handling, deadline management, notifications, audit trails, and reporting.
USER PMS is intended to improve correctness, transparency, compliance support, timely communication, auditability, and operational coordination while reducing manual effort.
USER PMS records and tracks patent-management information. Legal decisions, patent-office decisions, actual payment execution, external accounting operations, and external patent-office processing remain outside PMS unless explicitly recorded, validated, notified, routed, logged, or reported within PMS.
USER 2.2 Actors
USER 2.2.1 Applicant
USER Submits new patent applications with required documentation.
USER Tracks application status and progress.
USER Revises and resubmits applications based on feedback.
USER Manages co-inventor information and agreements.
USER Conducts prior-art searches through PMS-supported search functions.
USER Withdraws applications when withdrawal is allowed by current application status.
USER Responds to office actions with attorney support.
USER Views personal application reports.
USER 2.2.2 Director
USER Reviews assigned patent applications for technical merit.
USER Approves, rejects, or requests revisions to applications.
USER Provides detailed justification for decisions.
USER Reviews and approves budgets for patent filing and prosecution costs.
USER Makes final decisions on patent maintenance and renewal.
USER Views reports, dashboards, and analytics available within PMS.
USER Has oversight over application workflow in the Director domain.
USER 2.2.3 PCC_ADMIN
USER Assigns applications to Directors based on explicit assignment criteria.
USER Assigns attorneys from approved panels, subject to stated exceptions.
USER Coordinates PMS operations and monitors workflow.
USER Resolves or routes conflict-of-interest and exceptional cases within PMS scope.
USER Manages PMS-specific configuration and role assignments.
USER Generates system-wide PMS reports.
USER Maintains attorney panels and expertise mappings.
USER 2.2.4 Attorney
USER Conducts legal and patentability assessment.
USER Performs prior-art searches in relevant databases through PMS-supported search functions.
USER Provides legal opinions on patent applications.
USER Prepares applications for external filing.
USER Files applications with patent offices and records filing details in PMS.
USER Responds to office actions.
USER Manages post-grant tracking and maintenance-fee reminders/deadline tracking.
USER Provides legal advice throughout the patent lifecycle.
USER 2.3 Functional Scope
USER 2.3.1 Application Submission and Management
USER Applicants submit patent applications with required fields: title, abstract, detailed description, claims, inventor information, and supporting documents.
USER PMS validates that required application fields are complete before accepting submission.
USER PMS validates uploaded documents as PDF format, under 50 MB, and malware-free.
USER PMS classifies documents by sensitivity.
USER PMS assigns a unique application ID to each accepted application and sets the application status to Submitted.
USER Inventor agreements must be completed before filing.
USER PMS records application history and notifies relevant stakeholders.
USER 2.3.2 Application Assignment and Routing
USER After submission, PCC_ADMIN or PMS automatic routing assigns applications to Directors.
USER Assignment uses Director expertise matching, workload balance, and conflict-of-interest checks.
USER Directors or PCC_ADMIN cannot review applications where they are listed as inventors.
USER PMS updates the application status to Under Review upon assignment.
USER PMS notifies the assigned Director and initiates the applicable review timeline.
USER 2.3.3 Application Review and Decision
USER Directors review application details, documents, and supporting materials.
USER Directors must decide Approve, Reject, or Needs Revision.
USER Directors must provide detailed justification of at least 50 characters for all decisions.
USER Director review must be completed within 15 business days of assignment.
USER If an application is rejected or requires revision, PMS sends detailed feedback to the Applicant.
USER Approved applications proceed to attorney assignment.
USER PMS maintains an audit trail of the review process.
USER 2.3.4 Application Revision and Resubmission
USER Applicants may revise applications with status Needs Revision or Rejected.
USER Applicants must resubmit revised applications within the applicable 60-day resubmission period from the relevant rejection/revision decision date.
USER PMS validates that a revision addresses Director feedback.
USER PMS maintains version control for revised applications and starts a new review cycle after resubmission.
USER If the resubmission deadline expires, PMS changes the application status to Expired.
USER Applicants may withdraw applications before the filing stage where current status allows withdrawal.
USER 2.3.5 Attorney Assignment
USER Only PCC_ADMIN can assign attorneys to approved applications.
USER Attorneys must be selected from an approved panel unless an institutional exception is recorded or allowed.
USER Attorney assignment considers attorney expertise, workload, conflict-of-interest checks, and Applicant preference when the preferred attorney is in the approved panel.
USER The assigned Attorney must accept or decline the assignment.
USER PMS updates application status to Attorney Assigned or Pending Review as applicable.
USER 2.3.6 Patentability Assessment and Legal Review
USER Attorney conducts legal and patentability analysis.
USER Attorney performs prior-art search in relevant databases.
USER Attorney evaluates novelty, non-obviousness, and utility.
USER Attorney records a detailed patentability opinion with recommendation: File Patent, Do Not File, or Needs Amendment.
USER PMS records the opinion and notifies relevant stakeholders.
USER Attorney may request additional information or extensions as supported by PMS.
USER 2.3.7 Budget Approval and Financial Tracking
USER Director reviews and approves costs for patent filing and prosecution.
USER Budget information includes filing fees, attorney fees, and administrative costs.
USER If total cost exceeds the configured threshold limit, PMS routes/escalates the case to the applicable higher authority.
USER Budget approval is allowed only for applications with status Approved.
USER PMS validates budget availability before approval where budget availability information is available to PMS.
USER PMS logs financial transaction records and authorization levels for audit purposes.
USER Accounting, payment execution, and external finance-system processing are outside PMS unless recorded or integrated as PMS data.
USER 2.3.8 External Filing and Patent Office Communication
USER Attorney prepares the application package according to applicable patent-office requirements.
USER PMS validates format compliance with applicable patent-office standards where validation rules are available to PMS.
USER Attorney submits the application to the Indian Patent Office or an international patent office.
USER International filings require additional justification to be recorded in PMS.
USER PMS captures external filing ID, filing date, and confirmation information.
USER PMS updates application status to Filed after filing is recorded.
USER PMS logs official communications with patent offices.
USER 2.3.9 Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance
USER Post-grant tracking activates only for patents with status Granted.
USER PMS calculates maintenance-fee schedules based on grant date and jurisdiction where the applicable schedule rules are available to PMS.
USER Attorney manages maintenance-fee reminders and deadline tracking in PMS.
USER Before each maintenance deadline, an explicit renewal decision is required.
USER Attorney seeks renewal decision from the Director.
USER Director decides Renew or Do Not Renew.
USER If renewal is approved, Attorney processes the maintenance action and records the transaction/status in PMS.
USER PMS maintains renewal history and schedules the next maintenance cycle.
USER 2.3.10 Office Actions and Responses
USER PMS records office actions received from patent offices.
USER Attorney reviews office actions and prepares draft responses.
USER Applicant reviews and approves a response when the applicable process requires Applicant review.
USER Attorney finalizes and submits responses within applicable deadlines.
USER Attorney files extension requests when additional time is needed and the process allows extension.
USER PMS tracks response submission and updates status.
USER PMS automates timeline and deadline tracking for office-action responses where applicable deadline data is available.
USER 2.3.11 Application Withdrawal
USER Applicants may withdraw applications before the filing stage.
USER PMS validates that withdrawal is allowed based on current application status.
USER Applicant must provide a withdrawal reason.
USER PMS records co-inventor consent when the applicable process requires consent.
USER PMS updates application status to Withdrawn and removes the application from the active queue.
USER PMS maintains withdrawal history.
USER 2.3.12 Co-Inventor and Agreement Management
USER Applicants manage multiple inventors and ownership shares.
USER PMS validates inventor information and ownership percentages.
USER PMS collects and validates inventor consent and electronic signatures where legally permitted and configured.
USER Inventor agreements must be completed before filing.
USER PMS sends notifications to affected parties when inventor information changes.
USER 2.3.13 Prior Art Search
USER Applicants and Attorneys can search for prior-art references through PMS-supported search facilities.
USER PMS queries relevant patent and publication databases where integrations or search access are available.
USER PMS displays search results with relevance ranking.
USER Users can attach selected references to an application.
USER PMS logs search sessions for audit trail.
USER PMS supports keyword optimization and classification mapping as part of the search process.
USER 2.3.14 Document Version Management
USER PMS maintains version history of application documents.
USER Users can upload new document versions where their role and application status permit upload.
USER PMS applies automatic version numbering.
USER Previous document versions are archived and accessible according to role and status access rules.
USER PMS logs document modifications with user and timestamp.
USER Document access is controlled based on user role and application status.
USER Applicants can edit documents only when application status is Needs Revision.
USER 2.3.15 Patent Licensing and Technology Transfer
USER Applicants can submit licensing or technology-transfer requests.
USER PMS routes such requests to the appropriate committee or Director as stated by the applicable institutional process.
USER PMS tracks negotiation process information.
USER PMS records final agreements.
USER PMS updates or shares financial-system information where integration is available.
USER PMS maintains license records with confidentiality controls.
USER 2.3.16 Appeal Process
USER Applicants may file appeals against rejected applications.
USER Appeals must be filed within the applicable allowed time period.
USER Applicant provides grounds and justification for appeal.
USER Director reviews appeal and supporting documentation.
USER Director may Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, or Modify Decision.
USER PMS records the appeal outcome and initiates the next applicable process step.
USER 2.3.17 Deadline Management and Tracking
USER PMS calculates and monitors patent-management deadlines where deadline rules are available.
USER Deadlines include review deadlines, revision deadlines, office-action response deadlines, and maintenance-fee deadlines.
USER PMS generates alerts at configurable intervals, including 30 days, 15 days, and 5 days before deadline where applicable.
USER PMS sends deadline notifications to relevant stakeholders.
USER Missed deadlines trigger escalations according to the configured escalation chain.
USER PMS logs deadline-tracking activities.
USER 2.3.18 Reports and Analytics
USER PCC_ADMIN and Directors can generate PMS reports.
USER Available reports include application status and progress reports, financial and budget reports, deadline and pending-action reports, inventor and co-inventor reports, patent portfolio analysis, and workload and assignment reports.
USER PMS supports scheduled reports, exportable reports, and customizable reports as stated in the source baseline.
USER PMS logs report access for audit purposes.
USER PMS provides dashboards, historical data analysis, trend reporting, and workload analytics within the explicitly stated PMS reporting scope.
USER 2.3.19 Notifications
USER PMS issues system-generated email notifications for application submission and status changes.
USER PMS issues notifications for assignment to Director or Attorney.
USER PMS issues notifications for review decisions: approve, reject, or revision.
USER PMS issues notifications for budget approval or rejection.
USER PMS issues notifications for filing confirmations and office actions.
USER PMS issues notifications for deadline reminders and escalations.
USER PMS issues notifications for maintenance-fee reminders.
USER PMS issues notifications for document updates and version changes.
USER PMS notification triggering is within module scope; central email delivery infrastructure is outside PMS scope.
USER 2.3.20 Audit Trail and Compliance
USER PMS maintains audit trails of PMS actions.
USER PMS logs user actions, status changes, and document modifications.
USER PMS logs include user ID, action type, timestamp, application ID, previous state, and new state where applicable.
USER Critical operations create immutable audit records.
USER Audit logs support compliance verification and forensic analysis.
USER PMS supports document sensitivity classification, role-based access control, and version control for confidential patent information.
USER PMS applies data encryption at rest and in transit where this security requirement is implemented by the platform/module boundary.
USER 2.3.21 PMS Role, Workflow, Integration, and System Boundaries
USER Exactly one active PCC_ADMIN oversees PMS.
USER Multiple Directors handle reviews based on expertise domains.
USER Multiple Attorneys from approved panels conduct legal assessments.
USER Multiple Applicants submit applications.
USER PMS supports automated routing based on configurable rules, status-driven workflow, escalation mechanisms for delayed actions, and parallel processing where explicitly applicable.
USER PMS may integrate with patent office systems, financial systems, email notification systems, and document management systems where available.
USER External systems remain outside PMS unless PMS explicitly validates, routes, records, notifies, logs, or reports based on their data or events.
USER The System is not treated as a regular primary actor in later use cases derived from this problem statement. System behavior appears as internal validation, routing, status update, deadline calculation, notification, escalation, postcondition, maintained data, or reporting behavior.
USER 2.4 Scope Boundaries
USER PMS does not own HR master data, payroll, platform-wide user-account administration, procurement execution, payment execution, external accounting operations, patent-office decision making, external legal decisions, or external database operation.
USER PMS may reference external policies, laws, patent-office requirements, jurisdiction schedules, and institutional committees only where PMS explicitly validates, restricts, routes, records, notifies, escalates, or reports based on them.

USER 
USER Repository rules you must follow:
USER - There is no user table in this repository. The logged-in person is request.principal (fusion_auth.principal.Principal: user_id, username, kind, roles, permissions). Store people as integer ids (models.IntegerField), never as a ForeignKey to a user model.
USER - A module copies the shape of modules/placement: apps.py, registry.py, models.py, domain/ (pure Python, no Django imports), services/ (all writes), api/ (serializers, views, urls).
USER - The module code is "patent", and every permission code starts with "patent.".
USER - Every API view sets permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.<action>")], imported from fusion_auth.permissions.
USER - The four PMS actors are Applicant, Director, PCC_ADMIN and Attorney.

ASSISTANT class PatentApplication(TimeStampedModel):
ASSISTANT     """Core patent application record."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     STATUS_CHOICES = [
ASSISTANT         ("draft", "Draft"),
ASSISTANT         ("submitted", "Submitted"),
ASSISTANT         ("under_review", "Under Review"),
ASSISTANT         ("needs_revision", "Needs Revision"),
ASSISTANT         ("resubmitted", "Resubmitted"),
ASSISTANT         ("rejected", "Rejected"),
ASSISTANT         ("appeal_submitted", "Appeal Submitted"),
ASSISTANT         ("approved", "Approved"),
ASSISTANT         ("attorney_assigned", "Attorney Assigned"),
ASSISTANT         ("filed", "Filed"),
ASSISTANT         ("granted", "Granted"),
ASSISTANT         ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
ASSISTANT         ("expired", "Expired"),
ASSISTANT     ]
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     APPLICATION_NUMBER = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
ASSISTANT     title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
ASSISTANT     abstract = models.TextField(blank=True)
ASSISTANT     description = models.TextField()
ASSISTANT     claims = models.TextField()
ASSISTANT     technical_domain = models.CharField(max_length=100)
ASSISTANT

USER # PMS application state transitions (authoritative table, merged from all 16 basis workflows; read-only spec)
USER 
USER | From state | Actor | Use case | Guard / outcome | Resulting state |
USER |---|---|---|---|---|
USER 
USER | Draft / Not Submitted | Applicant | UC-PMS-001 / Submit Patent Application | Submission passes completeness/document validation | Submitted |
USER | Submitted | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-007 / Assign Application to Director | Eligible Director selected or valid automatic routing succeeds | Under Review |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Decision = Approve; justification >=50 characters; review within deadline | Approved |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Decision = Needs Revision; valid justification | Needs Revision |
USER | Needs Revision | Applicant | UC-PMS-005 / Revise and Resubmit Application | Within 60 days; revision addresses feedback | Resubmitted |
USER | Resubmitted | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-007 / Assign Application to Director | Valid assignment/reassignment | Under Review |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Decision = Reject; valid justification | Rejected |
USER | Rejected | Applicant | UC-PMS-022 / File Appeal Against Rejection | Appeal filed within allowed period with grounds/justification | Appeal Submitted |
USER | Appeal Submitted | Director | UC-PMS-023 / Review Appeal | Appeal outcome = Remand for Amendment | Remanded for Amendment |
USER | Appeal Submitted | Director | UC-PMS-023 / Review Appeal | Appeal outcome = Uphold Rejection | Rejection Upheld |
USER | Appeal Submitted | Director | UC-PMS-023 / Review Appeal | Appeal outcome = Modify Decision | Decision Modified |
USER | Approved | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-010 / Assign Attorney to Approved Application | Assignment satisfies panel/expertise/workload/conflict/preference rules | Assignment Offered |
USER | Assignment Offered | Attorney | UC-PMS-011 / Accept or Decline Attorney Assignment | Outcome = Accept | Attorney Assigned |
USER | Assignment Offered | Attorney | UC-PMS-011 / Accept or Decline Attorney Assignment | Outcome = Decline | Pending Review |
USER | Pending Review | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-010 / Assign Attorney to Approved Application | Valid reassignment to another eligible Attorney | Assignment Offered |
USER | Approved | Director | UC-PMS-009 / Review and Decide Patent Budget | Cost within applicable threshold; budget availability validated where data exists; outcome = Approve | Budget Approved |
USER | Approved | Director | UC-PMS-009 / Review and Decide Patent Budget | Outcome = Reject | Budget Rejected |
USER | Approved | Director | UC-PMS-009 / Review and Decide Patent Budget | Total cost exceeds configured threshold | Escalated |
USER | Escalated | PCC_ADMIN | UC-PMS-027 / Monitor Workflow and Handle Exceptional Cases | Handle/route the exceptional case within PMS scope | Escalation Tracked / External Decision Pending |
USER | Pre-Filing Preconditions Satisfied | Attorney | UC-PMS-013 / Prepare and Record External Patent Filing | Required approvals and budget complete; inventor agreements complete; applicable format rules satisfied; international justification if needed | Filed |
USER | Filed / Post-Filing Active | Attorney | UC-PMS-014 / Record Patent Office Communication | Official communication is an office action | Office Action Recorded |
USER | Office Action Recorded | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Applicant review is required; draft prepared | Awaiting Applicant Approval |
USER | Awaiting Applicant Approval | Applicant | UC-PMS-029 / Review Office-Action Response | Applicant records approval | Applicant Approved |
USER | Applicant Approved | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Response finalized and recorded submitted within deadline or permitted extension | Response Submitted |
USER | Office Action Recorded | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Applicant review not required; draft finalized | Response Ready |
USER | Response Ready | Attorney | UC-PMS-015 / Respond to Office Action | Response recorded submitted within deadline or permitted extension | Response Submitted |
USER | Granted / Maintenance Cycle Active | Attorney | UC-PMS-017 / Manage Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance | Maintenance decision is due | Renewal Decision Requested |
USER | Renewal Decision Requested | Director | UC-PMS-018 / Make Maintenance Renewal Decision | Outcome = Renew | Renewal Approved |
USER | Renewal Approved | Attorney | UC-PMS-017 / Manage Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance | Maintenance action recorded; next cycle scheduled | Renewed / Next Cycle Scheduled |
USER | Renewal Decision Requested | Director | UC-PMS-018 / Make Maintenance Renewal Decision | Outcome = Do Not Renew | Do Not Renew |
USER | Any configured withdrawal-eligible pre-filing state | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing; current status is withdrawal-eligible; reason provided; co-inventor consent if required | Withdrawn |
USER | Under Review | Director | UC-PMS-008 / Review Patent Application | Needs Revision | BR-PMS-029; BR-PMS-030; BR-PMS-031 |
USER | Renewal Decision Requested | Director | UC-PMS-018 / Make Maintenance Renewal Decision | Do Not Renew | BR-PMS-061 |
USER | Submitted | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Under Review | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Needs Revision | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Rejected | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Approved | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |
USER | Attorney Assigned | Applicant | UC-PMS-006 / Withdraw Patent Application | Before filing AND current state is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible | Withdrawn |

USER ../elm_specs_md/pms_kit/chunks/br_core.md

USER # PMS business rules for submission, assignment, review, revision, withdrawal, appeal, documents (read-only spec)
USER 
USER - BR-PMS-003 (Reporting; applies to Applicant): Applicants may access report information only for their own patent applications.
USER - BR-PMS-014 (Document validation; applies to Applicant): Application supporting documents accepted for submission must be PDF files.
USER - BR-PMS-015 (Document validation; applies to Applicant): An application document must be under 50 MB to be accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-016 (Document validation; applies to PMS): A submitted application document must pass malware validation before it is accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-017 (Application validation; applies to Applicant): Required application fields must be complete before PMS accepts the application submission.
USER - BR-PMS-020 (Agreement prerequisite; applies to Applicant / Attorney): Inventor agreements must be completed before external filing.
USER - BR-PMS-022 (Assignment; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Director): Automatic Director assignment may be used only when configured assignment rules are available, and it must apply the same governing assignment criteria. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-023 (Assignment rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Director expertise matching with the application domain is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-024 (Assignment rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Director workload balance is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-025 (Conflict of interest; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Director): PMS must perform a conflict-of-interest check before Director assignment.
USER - BR-PMS-026 (Conflict of interest; applies to Director): A Director must not review an application in which that Director is listed as an inventor.
USER - BR-PMS-027 (Conflict of interest; applies to PCC_ADMIN): PCC_ADMIN must not act as reviewer for an application in which PCC_ADMIN is listed as an inventor.
USER - BR-PMS-029 (Decision; applies to Director): A Director review decision must be exactly one of: Approve, Reject, or Needs Revision.
USER - BR-PMS-030 (Decision validation; applies to Director): Every Director review decision must include a justification of at least 50 characters.
USER - BR-PMS-031 (Review deadline; applies to Director): Director review must be completed within 15 business days of assignment.
USER - BR-PMS-033 (Revision; applies to Applicant): Application revision is permitted when the application status is Needs Revision.
USER - BR-PMS-034 (Revision; applies to Applicant): Application revision is permitted when the application status is Rejected.
USER - BR-PMS-035 (Revision deadline; applies to Applicant): A revised application must be resubmitted within 60 days from the relevant rejection or revision-request decision date.
USER - BR-PMS-036 (Revision validation; applies to Applicant / Director): A resubmitted revision must address the recorded Director feedback.
USER - BR-PMS-038 (Status update; applies to Applicant): When the applicable 60-day resubmission period expires without valid resubmission, PMS changes the application status to Expired.
USER - BR-PMS-067 (Withdrawal; applies to Applicant): Withdrawal is permitted only before the filing stage.
USER - BR-PMS-068 (Withdrawal validation; applies to Applicant): Withdrawal is permitted only when the current application status is withdrawal-eligible under the applicable PMS rule/configuration. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-069 (Withdrawal validation; applies to Applicant): A withdrawal request must include an Applicant-provided reason.
USER - BR-PMS-070 (Withdrawal consent; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Co-inventor consent for withdrawal must be recorded when the applicable process requires such consent.
USER - BR-PMS-073 (Inventor validation; applies to Applicant): Inventor information must pass PMS validation before the change is accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-074 (Ownership validation; applies to Applicant): Inventor ownership percentages must pass PMS validation before the change is accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-075 (Consent rule; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Required inventor consent must be obtained before filing.
USER - BR-PMS-076 (Electronic signature; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Electronic signatures for inventor consent/agreement may be collected only where legally permitted and configured. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-077 (Electronic signature validation; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Electronic-signature validation applies only where legally permitted and configured. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-078 (Agreement prerequisite; applies to Applicant / Attorney): Inventor agreements must be complete before filing.
USER - BR-PMS-082 (Document versioning; applies to Applicant / Attorney): PMS assigns automatic version numbers to new document versions.
USER - BR-PMS-084 (Document access; applies to Applicant / Director / Attorney): Access to previous document versions is limited by applicable role and application-status access rules.
USER - BR-PMS-086 (Access control; applies to Applicant / Director / Attorney / PCC_ADMIN): Document access is controlled by user role and application status.
USER - BR-PMS-087 (Document edit rule; applies to Applicant): Applicants may edit application documents only while the application status is Needs Revision.
USER - BR-PMS-092 (Appeal; applies to Applicant): Only an application in Rejected status is eligible for an Applicant appeal.
USER - BR-PMS-093 (Appeal deadline; applies to Applicant): An appeal must be filed within the applicable configured or institutionally defined allowed period.
USER - BR-PMS-094 (Appeal validation; applies to Applicant): An appeal must include Applicant-provided grounds and justification.
USER - BR-PMS-095 (Appeal decision; applies to Director): A Director appeal outcome must be exactly one of: Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, or Modify Decision.
USER - BR-PMS-126 (Access control; applies to Applicant / Director / PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): A valid PMS role assignment is required before a user may access patent-management functions.
USER - BR-PMS-130 (Confidentiality; applies to Applicant / Director / PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): Confidential patent information is protected by role-based access control.
USER - BR-PMS-140 (Scope boundary; applies to Document management system): Document-management systems are external; PMS owns only the document/version/access/audit behavior explicitly represented within PMS or an available integration.
USER ```

USER from django.db import models
USER from core.db.mixins import TimeStampedModel
USER 
USER 
USER class PatentApplication(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Core patent application record."""
USER     
USER     STATUS_CHOICES = [
USER         ("draft", "Draft"),
USER         ("submitted", "Submitted"),
USER         ("under_review", "Under Review"),
USER         ("needs_revision", "Needs Revision"),
USER         ("resubmitted", "Resubmitted"),
USER         ("rejected", "Rejected"),
USER         ("appeal_submitted", "Appeal Submitted"),
USER         ("approved", "Approved"),
USER         ("attorney_assigned", "Attorney Assigned"),
USER         ("filed", "Filed"),
USER         ("granted", "Granted"),
USER         ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
USER         ("expired", "Expired"),
USER     ]
USER 
USER     APPLICATION_NUMBER = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
USER     title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
USER     abstract = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     description = models.TextField()
USER     claims = models.TextField()
USER     technical_domain = models.CharField(max_length=100)
USER     
USER     applicant_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="draft")
USER     assigned_director_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     assigned_attorney_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     review_due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     resubmission_deadline = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     
USER     # Latest decision and justification (stored as text for flexibility)
USER     latest_decision = models.CharField(max_length=50, null=True, blank=True)
USER     latest_justification = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     
USER     version_number = models.IntegerField(default=1)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         ordering = ["-created_at"]
USER

USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION_NUMBER} ({self.status})"

USER     decision = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=[("renewal_requested", "Renewal Requested"), ("renew", "Renew"), ("do_not_renew", "Do Not Renew")], default="renewal_requested")
USER     decided_by = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION"]  # One decision per cycle (tracked by due_date)
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.due_date} ({self.decision})"
USER

USER class Appeal(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Appeal record against a rejection."""
USER

ASSISTANT # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
ASSISTANT # State Transitions (BR-PMS state table)
ASSISTANT # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {
ASSISTANT     "draft": {"submitted"},
ASSISTANT     "submitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
ASSISTANT     "under_review": {"approved", "needs_revision", "rejected", "escalated"},
ASSISTANT     "needs_revision": {"resubmitted", "withdrawn"},
ASSISTANT     "resubmitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
ASSISTANT     "rejected": {"appeal_submitted", "withdrawn"},
ASSISTANT     "appeal_submitted": {"remanded_for_amendment", "rejection_upheld", "decision_modified"},
ASSISTANT     "approved": {"attorney_assigned", "budget_approved", "budget_rejected", "budget_escalated"},
ASSISTANT     "attorney_assigned": {"attorney_declined", "pending_review"},
ASSISTANT     "pending_review": {"attorney_assigned"},  # Reassignment if declined or invalid
ASSISTANT     "filed": {"office_action_recorded"},  # Implicit transition on communication receipt
ASSISTANT     "office_action_recorded": {"awaiting_applicant_approval", "response_ready", "response_submitted"},
ASSISTANT     "awaiting_applicant_approval": {"applicant_approved"},
ASSISTANT     "applicant_approved": {"response_ready"},
ASSISTANT     "response_ready": {"response_submitted"},
ASSISTANT     "response_submitted": {"under_review"},  # Back to review cycle or granted
ASSISTANT     "granted": {"renewal_requested"},
ASSISTANT     "renewal_requested": {"renewed", "do_not_renew", "escalated"},
ASSISTANT     "renewed": {"next_cycle_scheduled"},
ASSISTANT     "withdrawn": {},
ASSISTANT     "expired": {},
ASSISTANT     "remanded_for_amendment": {"under_review"},  # Back to review after amendment

ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT def validate_document(file_name: str, content_type: str, size_bytes: int) -> bool:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Validate document for submission.
ASSISTANT     Rules: PDF only (BR-PMS-014), under 50 MB (BR-PMS-015).
ASSISTANT     Malware validation is assumed to be handled by an external service or middleware.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     if content_type.lower() != "application/pdf":
ASSISTANT         return False
ASSISTANT     if size_bytes > 50 * 1024 * 1024:  # 50 MB
ASSISTANT         return False
ASSISTANT     return True

ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Check for missing required application fields.
ASSISTANT     Required: title, abstract, description, claims, at least one inventor.
ASSISTANT     Returns list of missing field names.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     missing = []
ASSISTANT     if not data.get("title"):
ASSISTANT         missing.append("title")
ASSISTANT     if not data.get("abstract"):
ASSISTANT         missing.append("abstract")
ASSISTANT     if not data.get("description"):
ASSISTANT         missing.append("description")
ASSISTANT     if not data.get("claims"):
ASSISTANT         missing.append("claims")
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT     if len(inventors) == 0:
ASSISTANT         missing.append("inventor")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return missing

ASSISTANT def add_business_days(start_date: date, days: int) -> date:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Add business days to a start date.
ASSISTANT     Skips weekends (Saturday, Sunday).
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     current = start_date
ASSISTANT     count = 0
ASSISTANT     while count < days:
ASSISTANT         if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday (0) to Friday (4)
ASSISTANT             count += 1
ASSISTANT         current += timedelta(days=1)
ASSISTANT     return current
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT def can_withdraw(status: str) -> bool:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Check if withdrawal is allowed from the current status.
ASSISTANT     Rule BR-PMS-067: Only before filing stage.
ASSISTANT     Rule BR-PMS-068: Status must be withdrawal-eligible under PMS rules.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Withdrawal-eligible statuses based on state table and pre-filing constraint:
ASSISTANT     - draft, submitted, under_review, needs_revision, resubmitted, rejected
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Note: 'approved' is technically in the table but usually not withdrawable post-grant/filing.
ASSISTANT     However, strict interpretation of "before filing" suggests only very early stages.
ASSISTANT     Based on the state table provided, we allow withdrawal from states that are pre-filing or explicitly listed as eligible.
ASSISTANT     The state table lists 'submitted', 'under_review', etc. as having a transition to 'withdrawn'.
ASSISTANT     We will assume any status that has 'withdrawn' in its allowed transitions is eligible.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     return "withdrawn" in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(status, set())

ASSISTANT     Rule BR-PMS-009: If total cost exceeds threshold, status becomes 'Escalated'.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     return total > threshold

USER # PMS business rules for submission, assignment, review, revision, withdrawal, appeal, documents (read-only spec)
USER 
USER - BR-PMS-003 (Reporting; applies to Applicant): Applicants may access report information only for their own patent applications.
USER - BR-PMS-014 (Document validation; applies to Applicant): Application supporting documents accepted for submission must be PDF files.
USER - BR-PMS-015 (Document validation; applies to Applicant): An application document must be under 50 MB to be accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-016 (Document validation; applies to PMS): A submitted application document must pass malware validation before it is accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-017 (Application validation; applies to Applicant): Required application fields must be complete before PMS accepts the application submission.
USER - BR-PMS-020 (Agreement prerequisite; applies to Applicant / Attorney): Inventor agreements must be completed before external filing.
USER - BR-PMS-022 (Assignment; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Director): Automatic Director assignment may be used only when configured assignment rules are available, and it must apply the same governing assignment criteria. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-023 (Assignment rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Director expertise matching with the application domain is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-024 (Assignment rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Director workload balance is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-025 (Conflict of interest; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Director): PMS must perform a conflict-of-interest check before Director assignment.
USER - BR-PMS-026 (Conflict of interest; applies to Director): A Director must not review an application in which that Director is listed as an inventor.
USER - BR-PMS-027 (Conflict of interest; applies to PCC_ADMIN): PCC_ADMIN must not act as reviewer for an application in which PCC_ADMIN is listed as an inventor.
USER - BR-PMS-029 (Decision; applies to Director): A Director review decision must be exactly one of: Approve, Reject, or Needs Revision.
USER - BR-PMS-030 (Decision validation; applies to Director): Every Director review decision must include a justification of at least 50 characters.
USER - BR-PMS-031 (Review deadline; applies to Director): Director review must be completed within 15 business days of assignment.
USER - BR-PMS-033 (Revision; applies to Applicant): Application revision is permitted when the application status is Needs Revision.
USER - BR-PMS-034 (Revision; applies to Applicant): Application revision is permitted when the application status is Rejected.
USER - BR-PMS-035 (Revision deadline; applies to Applicant): A revised application must be resubmitted within 60 days from the relevant rejection or revision-request decision date.
USER - BR-PMS-036 (Revision validation; applies to Applicant / Director): A resubmitted revision must address the recorded Director feedback.
USER - BR-PMS-038 (Status update; applies to Applicant): When the applicable 60-day resubmission period expires without valid resubmission, PMS changes the application status to Expired.
USER - BR-PMS-067 (Withdrawal; applies to Applicant): Withdrawal is permitted only before the filing stage.
USER - BR-PMS-068 (Withdrawal validation; applies to Applicant): Withdrawal is permitted only when the current application status is withdrawal-eligible under the applicable PMS rule/configuration. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-069 (Withdrawal validation; applies to Applicant): A withdrawal request must include an Applicant-provided reason.
USER - BR-PMS-070 (Withdrawal consent; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Co-inventor consent for withdrawal must be recorded when the applicable process requires such consent.
USER - BR-PMS-073 (Inventor validation; applies to Applicant): Inventor information must pass PMS validation before the change is accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-074 (Ownership validation; applies to Applicant): Inventor ownership percentages must pass PMS validation before the change is accepted.
USER - BR-PMS-075 (Consent rule; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Required inventor consent must be obtained before filing.
USER - BR-PMS-076 (Electronic signature; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Electronic signatures for inventor consent/agreement may be collected only where legally permitted and configured. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-077 (Electronic signature validation; applies to Applicant / Co-inventor): Electronic-signature validation applies only where legally permitted and configured. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-078 (Agreement prerequisite; applies to Applicant / Attorney): Inventor agreements must be complete before filing.
USER - BR-PMS-082 (Document versioning; applies to Applicant / Attorney): PMS assigns automatic version numbers to new document versions.
USER - BR-PMS-084 (Document access; applies to Applicant / Director / Attorney): Access to previous document versions is limited by applicable role and application-status access rules.
USER - BR-PMS-086 (Access control; applies to Applicant / Director / Attorney / PCC_ADMIN): Document access is controlled by user role and application status.
USER - BR-PMS-087 (Document edit rule; applies to Applicant): Applicants may edit application documents only while the application status is Needs Revision.
USER - BR-PMS-092 (Appeal; applies to Applicant): Only an application in Rejected status is eligible for an Applicant appeal.
USER - BR-PMS-093 (Appeal deadline; applies to Applicant): An appeal must be filed within the applicable configured or institutionally defined allowed period.
USER - BR-PMS-094 (Appeal validation; applies to Applicant): An appeal must include Applicant-provided grounds and justification.
USER - BR-PMS-095 (Appeal decision; applies to Director): A Director appeal outcome must be exactly one of: Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, or Modify Decision.
USER - BR-PMS-126 (Access control; applies to Applicant / Director / PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): A valid PMS role assignment is required before a user may access patent-management functions.
USER - BR-PMS-130 (Confidentiality; applies to Applicant / Director / PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): Confidential patent information is protected by role-based access control.
USER - BR-PMS-140 (Scope boundary; applies to Document management system): Document-management systems are external; PMS owns only the document/version/access/audit behavior explicitly represented within PMS or an available integration.
USER ```

USER class PatentApplication(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Core patent application record."""
USER     
USER     STATUS_CHOICES = [
USER         ("draft", "Draft"),
USER         ("submitted", "Submitted"),
USER         ("under_review", "Under Review"),
USER         ("needs_revision", "Needs Revision"),
USER         ("resubmitted", "Resubmitted"),
USER         ("rejected", "Rejected"),
USER         ("appeal_submitted", "Appeal Submitted"),
USER         ("approved", "Approved"),
USER         ("attorney_assigned", "Attorney Assigned"),
USER         ("filed", "Filed"),
USER         ("granted", "Granted"),
USER         ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
USER         ("expired", "Expired"),
USER     ]
USER 
USER     APPLICATION_NUMBER = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
USER     title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
USER     abstract = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     description = models.TextField()
USER     claims = models.TextField()
USER     technical_domain = models.CharField(max_length=100)
USER     
USER     applicant_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="draft")
USER     assigned_director_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     assigned_attorney_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     review_due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     resubmission_deadline = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER

USER     latest_justification = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     
USER     version_number = models.IntegerField(default=1)

USER         app_label = "patent"

USER modules/patent/domain/policy.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Pure Python policy rules for the Patent Management System (PMS).
USER No Django, DRF, or Celery imports.
USER Enforces business rules BR-PMS-003 through BR-PMS-140 as specified in read-only specs.
USER """
USER 
USER from dataclasses import dataclass
USER from datetime import date, timedelta
USER from typing import Dict, Set, List, Any
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # State Transitions (BR-PMS state table)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {
USER     "draft": {"submitted"},
USER     "submitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
USER     "under_review": {"approved", "needs_revision", "rejected", "escalated"},
USER     "needs_revision": {"resubmitted", "withdrawn"},
USER     "resubmitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
USER     "rejected": {"appeal_submitted", "withdrawn"},
USER     "appeal_submitted": {"remanded_for_amendment", "rejection_upheld", "decision_modified"},
USER     "approved": {"attorney_assigned", "budget_approved", "budget_rejected", "budget_escalated"},
USER     "attorney_assigned": {"attorney_declined", "pending_review"},
USER     "pending_review": {"attorney_assigned"},  # Reassignment if declined or invalid
USER     "filed": {"office_action_recorded"},  # Implicit transition on communication receipt
USER     "office_action_recorded": {"awaiting_applicant_approval", "response_ready", "response_submitted"},
USER     "awaiting_applicant_approval": {"applicant_approved"},
USER     "applicant_approved": {"response_ready"},
USER     "response_ready": {"response_submitted"},
USER     "response_submitted": {"under_review"},  # Back to review cycle or granted
USER     "granted": {"renewal_requested"},
USER     "renewal_requested": {"renewed", "do_not_renew", "escalated"},
USER     "renewed": {"next_cycle_scheduled"},
USER     "withdrawn": {},
USER     "expired": {},
USER     "remanded_for_amendment": {"under_review"},  # Back to review after amendment
USER     "decision_modified": {"under_review"},  # Back to review after modification
USER }
USER 
USER 
USER def can_transition(from_status: str, to_status: str) -> bool:
USER     """Check if a transition is allowed based on the state table."""
USER     return to_status in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(from_status, set())
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Document Validation (BR-PMS-014, 015, 016)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def validate_document(file_name: str, content_type: str, size_bytes: int) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Validate document for submission.
USER     Rules: PDF only (BR-PMS-014), under 50 MB (BR-PMS-015).
USER     Malware validation is assumed to be handled by an external service or middleware.
USER     """
USER     if content_type.lower() != "application/pdf":
USER         return False
USER     if size_bytes > 50 * 1024 * 1024:  # 50 MB
USER         return False
USER     return True
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Application Validation (BR-PMS-017)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def missing_required_fields(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
USER     """
USER     Check for missing required application fields.
USER     Required: title, abstract, description, claims, at least one inventor.
USER     Returns list of missing field names.
USER     """
USER     missing = []
USER     if not data.get("title"):
USER         missing.append("title")
USER     if not data.get("abstract"):
USER         missing.append("abstract")
USER     if not data.get("description"):
USER         missing.append("description")
USER     if not data.get("claims"):
USER         missing.append("claims")
USER     
USER     inventors = data.get("inventors", [])
USER     if len(inventors) == 0:
USER         missing.append("inventor")
USER     
USER     return missing
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Decision Validation (BR-PMS-029, 030)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def validate_justification(text: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Validate Director review justification.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-030: Must be at least 50 characters.
USER     """
USER     return len(text.strip()) >= 50
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Date Calculations (BR-PMS-031, 035)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def add_business_days(start_date: date, days: int) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Add business days to a start date.
USER     Skips weekends (Saturday, Sunday).
USER     """
USER     current = start_date
USER     count = 0
USER     while count < days:
USER         if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday (0) to Friday (4)
USER             count += 1
USER         current += timedelta(days=1)
USER     return current
USER 
USER 
USER def review_due_date(assigned_on: date) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the review due date for a Director.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-031: 15 business days from assignment.
USER     """
USER     return add_business_days(assigned_on, 15)
USER 
USER 
USER def resubmission_deadline(decision_date: date) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the resubmission deadline for an Applicant.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-035: 60 days from rejection or revision-request decision.
USER     """
USER     return add_business_days(decision_date, 60)
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Conflict of Interest (BR-PMS-025, 026, 027)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER @dataclass(frozen=True)
USER class InventorInfo:
USER     user_id: int
USER     name: str
USER 
USER 
USER def is_conflicted(reviewer_id: int, inventor_user_ids: Set[int]) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if a reviewer is conflicted with any inventor.
USER     Rules BR-PMS-025 (PCC_ADMIN), BR-PMS-026 (Director):
USER     A reviewer listed as an inventor cannot review the application.
USER     """
USER     return reviewer_id in inventor_user_ids
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Withdrawal Eligibility (BR-PMS-067, 068)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def can_withdraw(status: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if withdrawal is allowed from the current status.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-067: Only before filing stage.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-068: Status must be withdrawal-eligible under PMS rules.
USER     
USER     Withdrawal-eligible statuses based on state table and pre-filing constraint:
USER     - draft, submitted, under_review, needs_revision, resubmitted, rejected
USER     
USER     Note: 'approved' is technically in the table but usually not withdrawable post-grant/filing.
USER     However, strict interpretation of "before filing" suggests only very early stages.
USER     Based on the state table provided, we allow withdrawal from states that are pre-filing or explicitly listed as eligible.
USER     The state table lists 'submitted', 'under_review', etc. as having a transition to 'withdrawn'.
USER     We will assume any status that has 'withdrawn' in its allowed transitions is eligible.
USER     """
USER     return "withdrawn" in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(status, set())
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Budget Escalation (BR-PMS-009)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def budget_needs_escalation(total: float, threshold: float) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if a budget total exceeds the configured threshold.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-009: If total cost exceeds threshold, status becomes 'Escalated'.
USER     """
USER     return total > threshold
USER ```

USER ../elm_specs_md/pms_kit/chunks/uc_core.md
USER ```
USER # PMS use cases: submission, Director review, revision, withdrawal, appeal, documents (read-only spec)
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-001 — Submit Patent Application
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: Create and submit a complete patent application for institutional processing.
USER Preconditions: Applicant has a valid PMS role. A submission draft can be created.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Applicant enters title, abstract, detailed description, claims, inventor information, and supporting documents.
USER   2. PMS validates required-field completeness and validates each submitted document as PDF, under 50 MB, and malware-free.
USER   3. PMS classifies accepted documents by sensitivity.
USER   4. Applicant confirms submission.
USER   5. PMS accepts the application, assigns a unique application ID, sets status to Submitted, records history/audit data, and triggers the submission notification.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • If required information is incomplete, PMS identifies the missing information and keeps the application unsubmitted.
USER   • If a document fails format, size, or malware validation, PMS rejects that document and keeps the valid prior application state.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If an external validation dependency is unavailable, PMS does not treat the application as successfully accepted unless the required validation has completed.
USER Postcondition: An accepted application has a unique ID and status Submitted; its submission/history/audit record exists.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-014; BR-PMS-015; BR-PMS-016; BR-PMS-017; BR-PMS-126; BR-PMS-130
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-002 — Track Application Status and History
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: View the current status, progress, and recorded history of the Applicant's own applications.
USER Preconditions: Applicant has a valid PMS role and access to the selected application.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Applicant selects one of their patent applications.
USER   2. PMS displays current status/progress and the recorded application history available to the Applicant.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Applicant may navigate among their own applications and available history entries.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If the Applicant is not authorized for the selected record, PMS blocks access.
USER Postcondition: The Applicant has viewed current status/progress/history; no business state changes.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-003; BR-PMS-126; BR-PMS-130
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-003 — Manage Co-Inventors and Agreements
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: Maintain inventor identities, ownership shares, consents, and agreements for an application.
USER Preconditions: Applicant has access to the application and the current role/status permits the requested change.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Applicant adds or updates inventor information and ownership shares.
USER   2. PMS validates inventor information and ownership percentages.
USER   3. Where required, PMS records inventor consent and, where legally permitted and configured, collects/validates electronic signatures.
USER   4. PMS records agreement information and notifies affected parties when inventor information changes.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Applicant may update the inventor set or ownership shares before filing, subject to applicable access/state rules.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • Invalid inventor information, ownership percentages, consent, or signature validation prevents the affected change from being accepted.
USER Postcondition: Valid inventor/agreement information is stored; agreement-completion state is available for the filing prerequisite.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-020; BR-PMS-073; BR-PMS-074; BR-PMS-075; BR-PMS-076; BR-PMS-077; BR-PMS-078; BR-PMS-126; BR-PMS-130
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-005 — Revise and Resubmit Application
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: Revise an application after a Needs Revision or Rejected decision and resubmit it for a new review cycle.
USER Preconditions: Application status is Needs Revision or Rejected; the 60-day resubmission period has not expired.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Applicant reviews the Director feedback.
USER   2. Applicant edits permitted application content/documents and uploads revised versions.
USER   3. PMS validates that the resubmission addresses the Director feedback and applies document/version rules.
USER   4. Applicant submits the revision.
USER   5. PMS records the revised version and starts a new review cycle.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • The Applicant may save revisions before final resubmission while the permitted revision state remains active.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If the deadline has expired, PMS changes the application to Expired and does not accept a late resubmission.
USER   • If the submitted revision does not address the recorded Director feedback, PMS rejects the resubmission and retains the prior valid state.
USER Postcondition: A revised application is recorded for a new review cycle.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-033; BR-PMS-034; BR-PMS-035; BR-PMS-036; BR-PMS-038; BR-PMS-082; BR-PMS-087; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-006 — Withdraw Patent Application
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: Withdraw an application when the applicable pre-filing status permits withdrawal.
USER Preconditions: Application has not reached Filed status and the current application status is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PMS checks that withdrawal is allowed for the current application status and before filing.
USER   2. Applicant provides a withdrawal reason.
USER   3. Where the applicable process requires it, PMS records co-inventor consent.
USER   4. Applicant confirms withdrawal.
USER   5. PMS sets status to Withdrawn, removes the application from the active queue, and records withdrawal history/status change.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • None beyond the conditional co-inventor-consent requirement.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If the current status is not withdrawal-eligible, PMS blocks withdrawal and retains the current state.
USER Postcondition: The application is Withdrawn and no longer appears in the active queue.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-067; BR-PMS-068; BR-PMS-069; BR-PMS-070; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-007 — Assign Application to Director
USER Actor: PCC_ADMIN
USER Goal: Assign a Submitted application to an eligible Director and start the review timeline.
USER Preconditions: Application status is Submitted; PCC_ADMIN is authorized, or configured automatic routing is enabled.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PMS/PCC_ADMIN evaluates Director expertise, workload, and conflict-of-interest constraints.
USER   2. PMS prevents assignment to a Director who is listed as an inventor; PCC_ADMIN cannot act as reviewer where they are an inventor.
USER   3. PCC_ADMIN confirms the selected Director, or PMS performs configured automatic routing using the same rules.
USER   4. PMS records the assignment, sets status to Under Review, notifies the Director, and initiates the review deadline.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Automatic routing may perform the assignment without treating the System as a primary actor.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If no eligible assignment is available, PMS leaves the application Submitted and records/raises the exceptional case for PCC_ADMIN handling.
USER Postcondition: The application is assigned and status is Under Review, or remains Submitted if assignment is blocked.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-022; BR-PMS-023; BR-PMS-024; BR-PMS-025; BR-PMS-026; BR-PMS-027; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-008 — Review Patent Application
USER Actor: Director
USER Goal: Review an assigned application and record one supported technical-merit decision.
USER Preconditions: Director is the assigned, non-conflicted reviewer; the application is Under Review.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Director reviews application details, documents, and supporting materials.
USER   2. Director selects exactly one outcome: Approve, Reject, or Needs Revision.
USER   3. Director enters a detailed justification of at least 50 characters.
USER   4. Director submits the decision within the 15-business-day review period.
USER   5. PMS records the decision/audit trail. Approved applications become eligible for attorney assignment; Reject/Needs Revision decisions trigger detailed feedback to the Applicant.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Reject and Needs Revision are valid alternative outcomes to Approve.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • A decision without the required justification is not accepted.
USER   • A conflicted/unassigned Director is not allowed to submit the review decision.
USER Postcondition: One review outcome is recorded with justification and audit history.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-026; BR-PMS-029; BR-PMS-030; BR-PMS-031; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-019 — Manage Document Versions
USER Actor: Applicant (also Attorney/Director/PCC_ADMIN when authorized)
USER Goal: Upload, version, archive, and access application documents under role/status controls.
USER Preconditions: User has a valid PMS role; requested action is permitted for the role and application status.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. User uploads a new document version where permitted.
USER   2. PMS assigns the next version number and archives the previous version.
USER   3. PMS records the modification user and timestamp.
USER   4. Authorized users may access previous versions according to role/status rules.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Applicants may edit application documents only in Needs Revision status.
USER   • Other roles may upload/access versions only where their role/status permission permits.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • Unauthorized upload/access is blocked and logged according to applicable audit/security behavior.
USER Postcondition: Document version history and access metadata are updated consistently.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-082; BR-PMS-084; BR-PMS-086; BR-PMS-087; BR-PMS-130; BR-PMS-140; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-022 — File Appeal Against Rejection
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: Submit a supported appeal against a Rejected application within the applicable appeal period.
USER Preconditions: Application is Rejected; the configured/institutional appeal period has not expired.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Applicant enters grounds and justification for appeal.
USER   2. Applicant submits the appeal.
USER   3. PMS validates appeal eligibility/timing and records the appeal for Director review.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • None.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • An appeal submitted for a non-Rejected application or outside the allowed period is not accepted.
USER Postcondition: An eligible appeal is recorded as submitted for review.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-092; BR-PMS-093; BR-PMS-094; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-023 — Review Appeal
USER Actor: Director
USER Goal: Review an appeal and record one permitted appeal outcome.
USER Preconditions: Appeal exists and is assigned/available for Director review.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Director reviews the appeal and supporting documentation.
USER   2. Director selects one outcome: Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, or Modify Decision.
USER   3. PMS records the appeal outcome and initiates the next applicable process step without inventing unspecified legal/process details.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • The three listed outcomes are alternative success results.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • An unsupported appeal outcome is not accepted.
USER Postcondition: Appeal outcome is recorded and the corresponding next-step state is initiated where defined.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-095; BR-PMS-126
USER ```

ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Missing required fields: {', '.join(missing)}")

ASSISTANT         if not validate_document(doc['file_name'], doc.get('content_type', ''), doc.get('size', 0)):
ASSISTANT             invalid_docs.append(doc['file_name'])
ASSISTANT     if invalid_docs:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Invalid documents: {', '.join(invalid_docs)}")

ASSISTANT             'applicant_id': applicant_id,
ASSISTANT             'status': 'submitted',
ASSISTANT             'assigned_director_id': None,
ASSISTANT             'assigned_attorney_id': None,

ASSISTANT         # Update existing application if it was found (though get_or_create with unique constraint usually prevents this unless logic differs)

ASSISTANT def assign_director(admin_id: int, application_id: int, director_id: int) -> PatentApplication:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Assign a Director to review an application.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Checks for conflict of interest (BR-PMS-025, 026, 027).
ASSISTANT     Sets review due date and status to Under Review.
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT     """

ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is listed as an inventor and cannot review this application.")

ASSISTANT     app.assigned_director_id = director_id
ASSISTANT     app.status = 'under_review'

ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Record history
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=app,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=admin_id,

ASSISTANT def review_application(director_id: int, application_id: int, decision: str, justification: str) -> PatentApplication:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Record a Director's review decision.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Only the assigned Director can submit.
ASSISTANT     Decision must be one of: Approve, Reject, Needs Revision.
ASSISTANT     Justification must be at least 50 characters (BR-PMS-030).
ASSISTANT     Sets resubmission deadline for Reject/Needs Revision (BR-PMS-035).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises ValueError if director is not assigned, decision invalid, or justification too short.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check if director is assigned
ASSISTANT     if app.assigned_director_id != director_id:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is not assigned to this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Validate decision
ASSISTANT     valid_decisions = ['approve', 'reject', 'needs_revision']
ASSISTANT     if decision not in valid_decisions:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision. Must be one of: {', '.join(valid_decisions)}")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Validate justification length
ASSISTANT     if not validate_justification(justification):
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError("Justification must be at least 50 characters.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update application
ASSISTANT     app.latest_decision = decision
ASSISTANT     app.latest_justification = justification
ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Set resubmission deadline if needed
ASSISTANT     if decision in ['reject', 'needs_revision']:
ASSISTANT         app.resubmission_deadline = resubmission_deadline(app.created_at)  # Using created_at as reference; adjust if decision_date is tracked separately
ASSISTANT         app.status = 'rejected' if decision == 'reject' else 'needs_revision'
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Record history
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=app,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=director_id,
ASSISTANT         action='review',
ASSISTANT         from_status=app.status,
ASSISTANT         to_status=app.status,
ASSISTANT         note=f"Decision: {decision}"
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return app

ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check ownership
ASSISTANT     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check status eligibility
ASSISTANT     if app.status not in ['needs_revision', 'rejected']:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Application status {app.status} is not eligible for resubmission.")
ASSISTANT
