ASSISTANT     if app.resubmission_deadline and app.resubmission_deadline < date.today():
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Resubmission deadline ({app.resubmission_deadline}) has passed.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update application
ASSISTANT     app.version_number += 1
ASSISTANT     app.status = 'resubmitted'
ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Record history
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=app,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=applicant_id,
ASSISTANT         action='resubmit',
ASSISTANT         from_status=app.status,
ASSISTANT         to_status='resubmitted',
ASSISTANT         note=f"Resubmission with version {app.version_number}. Feedback addressed."
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return app

ASSISTANT def withdraw_application(applicant_id: int, application_id: int, reason: str) -> PatentApplication:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Withdraw an application.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Only the owner can withdraw.
ASSISTANT     Only allowed if policy.can_withdraw returns True (BR-PMS-067, 068).
ASSISTANT     Requires co-inventor consent if applicable (simplified check here).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises ValueError if actor is not owner or withdrawal not allowed.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check ownership
ASSISTANT     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check withdrawal eligibility
ASSISTANT     if not can_withdraw(app.status):
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Withdrawal is not allowed from status {app.status}.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update application
ASSISTANT     app.status = 'withdrawn'
ASSISTANT     app.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Record history
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=app,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=applicant_id,
ASSISTANT         action='withdraw',
ASSISTANT         from_status=app.status,
ASSISTANT         to_status='withdrawn',
ASSISTANT         note=f"Application withdrawn by applicant. Reason: {reason}"
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return app
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def expire_overdue_resubmissions(today: date) -> List[PatentApplication]:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Set status to Expired for applications where resubmission deadline has passed without valid resubmission (BR-PMS-038).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Returns list of expired applications.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     expired_apps = []
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Find applications with a past resubmission deadline and not yet resubmitted/resolved
ASSISTANT     apps_to_expire = PatentApplication.objects.filter(
ASSISTANT         resubmission_deadline__lt=today,
ASSISTANT         status__in=['needs_revision', 'rejected']  # Only these statuses can lead to expiration via missed deadline
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     for app in apps_to_expire:
ASSISTANT         if app.status not in ['resubmitted', 'approved', 'granted', 'withdrawn']:
ASSISTANT             app.status = 'expired'
ASSISTANT             app.save()
ASSISTANT             expired_apps.append(app)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT             # Record history
ASSISTANT             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT                 application=app,
ASSISTANT                 actor_id=None,  # System action
ASSISTANT                 action='expire',
ASSISTANT                 from_status=app.status,
ASSISTANT                 to_status='expired',
ASSISTANT                 note=f"Resubmission deadline passed without valid resubmission."
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return expired_apps
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def file_appeal(applicant_id: int, application_id: int, grounds: str) -> Appeal:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     File an appeal against a rejection.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Only the owner can file.
ASSISTANT     Application must be in 'rejected' status (BR-PMS-092).
ASSISTANT     Must be within allowed period (simplified check here).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises ValueError if actor is not owner, application not rejected, or outside appeal period.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check ownership
ASSISTANT     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check status eligibility
ASSISTANT     if app.status != 'rejected':
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Application must be in 'rejected' status to file an appeal. Current status: {app.status}.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Create appeal record
ASSISTANT     appeal = Appeal.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=app,
ASSISTANT         grounds=grounds,
ASSISTANT         status='submitted',
ASSISTANT         decided_by=None,
ASSISTANT         justification=''  # Can be populated later if needed
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return appeal
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def decide_appeal(director_id: int, appeal_id: int, outcome: str, justification: str) -> Appeal:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Record a Director's decision on an appeal.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Only the assigned Director can decide (simplified assumption that director is linked to appeal or application).
ASSISTANT     Outcome must be one of: Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, Modify Decision (BR-PMS-095).
ASSISTANT     Justification required (simplified check here).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises ValueError if outcome invalid or justification missing.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     appeal = Appeal.objects.get(id=appeal_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check if director is the one who decided previously (for simplicity, assume director_id matches application's assigned_director_id)
ASSISTANT     app = appeal.application
ASSISTANT     if app.assigned_director_id != director_id:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is not assigned to this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Validate outcome
ASSISTANT     valid_outcomes = ['uphold_rejection', 'remand_for_amendment', 'modify_decision']
ASSISTANT     if outcome not in valid_outcomes:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Invalid appeal outcome. Must be one of: {', '.join(valid_outcomes)}")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update appeal
ASSISTANT     appeal.status = outcome.replace('_', '_')  # Map string to status field value (adjust mapping as needed)
ASSISTANT     appeal.decided_by = director_id
ASSISTANT     appeal.justification = justification
ASSISTANT     appeal.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return appeal

USER """
USER Pure Python policy rules for the Patent Management System (PMS).
USER No Django, DRF, or Celery imports.
USER Enforces business rules BR-PMS-003 through BR-PMS-140 as specified in read-only specs.
USER """
USER 
USER from dataclasses import dataclass
USER from datetime import date, timedelta
USER from typing import Dict, Set, List, Any
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # State Transitions (BR-PMS state table)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {
USER     "draft": {"submitted"},
USER     "submitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
USER     "under_review": {"approved", "needs_revision", "rejected", "escalated"},
USER     "needs_revision": {"resubmitted", "withdrawn"},
USER     "resubmitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
USER     "rejected": {"appeal_submitted", "withdrawn"},
USER     "appeal_submitted": {"remanded_for_amendment", "rejection_upheld", "decision_modified"},
USER     "approved": {"attorney_assigned", "budget_approved", "budget_rejected", "budget_escalated"},
USER     "attorney_assigned": {"attorney_declined", "pending_review"},
USER     "pending_review": {"attorney_assigned"},  # Reassignment if declined or invalid
USER     "filed": {"office_action_recorded"},  # Implicit transition on communication receipt
USER     "office_action_recorded": {"awaiting_applicant_approval", "response_ready", "response_submitted"},
USER     "awaiting_applicant_approval": {"applicant_approved"},
USER     "applicant_approved": {"response_ready"},
USER     "response_ready": {"response_submitted"},
USER     "response_submitted": {"under_review"},  # Back to review cycle or granted
USER     "granted": {"renewal_requested"},
USER     "renewal_requested": {"renewed", "do_not_renew", "escalated"},
USER     "renewed": {"next_cycle_scheduled"},
USER     "withdrawn": {},
USER     "expired": {},
USER     "remanded_for_amendment": {"under_review"},  # Back to review after amendment
USER     "decision_modified": {"under_review"},  # Back to review after modification
USER }
USER 
USER 
USER def can_transition(from_status: str, to_status: str) -> bool:
USER     """Check if a transition is allowed based on the state table."""
USER     return to_status in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(from_status, set())
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Document Validation (BR-PMS-014, 015, 016)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def validate_document(file_name: str, content_type: str, size_bytes: int) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Validate document for submission.
USER     Rules: PDF only (BR-PMS-014), under 50 MB (BR-PMS-015).
USER     Malware validation is assumed to be handled by an external service or middleware.
USER     """
USER     if content_type.lower() != "application/pdf":
USER         return False
USER     if size_bytes > 50 * 1024 * 1024:  # 50 MB
USER         return False
USER     return True
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Application Validation (BR-PMS-017)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def missing_required_fields(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
USER     """
USER     Check for missing required application fields.
USER     Required: title, abstract, description, claims, at least one inventor.
USER     Returns list of missing field names.
USER     """
USER     missing = []
USER     if not data.get("title"):
USER         missing.append("title")
USER     if not data.get("abstract"):
USER         missing.append("abstract")
USER     if not data.get("description"):
USER         missing.append("description")
USER     if not data.get("claims"):
USER         missing.append("claims")
USER     
USER     inventors = data.get("inventors", [])
USER     if len(inventors) == 0:
USER         missing.append("inventor")
USER     
USER     return missing
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Decision Validation (BR-PMS-029, 030)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def validate_justification(text: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Validate Director review justification.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-030: Must be at least 50 characters.
USER     """
USER     return len(text.strip()) >= 50
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Date Calculations (BR-PMS-031, 035)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def add_business_days(start_date: date, days: int) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Add business days to a start date.
USER     Skips weekends (Saturday, Sunday).
USER     """
USER     current = start_date
USER     count = 0
USER     while count < days:
USER         if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday (0) to Friday (4)
USER             count += 1
USER         current += timedelta(days=1)
USER     return current
USER 
USER 
USER def review_due_date(assigned_on: date) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the review due date for a Director.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-031: 15 business days from assignment.
USER     """
USER     return add_business_days(assigned_on, 15)
USER 
USER 
USER def resubmission_deadline(decision_date: date) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the resubmission deadline for an Applicant.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-035: 60 days from rejection or revision-request decision.
USER     """
USER     return add_business_days(decision_date, 60)
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Conflict of Interest (BR-PMS-025, 026, 027)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER @dataclass(frozen=True)
USER class InventorInfo:
USER     user_id: int
USER     name: str
USER 
USER 
USER def is_conflicted(reviewer_id: int, inventor_user_ids: Set[int]) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if a reviewer is conflicted with any inventor.
USER     Rules BR-PMS-025 (PCC_ADMIN), BR-PMS-026 (Director):
USER     A reviewer listed as an inventor cannot review the application.
USER     """
USER     return reviewer_id in inventor_user_ids
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Withdrawal Eligibility (BR-PMS-067, 068)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def can_withdraw(status: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if withdrawal is allowed from the current status.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-067: Only before filing stage.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-068: Status must be withdrawal-eligible under PMS rules.
USER     
USER     Withdrawal-eligible statuses based on state table and pre-filing constraint:
USER     - draft, submitted, under_review, needs_revision, resubmitted, rejected
USER     
USER     Note: 'approved' is technically in the table but usually not withdrawable post-grant/filing.
USER     However, strict interpretation of "before filing" suggests only very early stages.
USER     Based on the state table provided, we allow withdrawal from states that are pre-filing or explicitly listed as eligible.
USER     The state table lists 'submitted', 'under_review', etc. as having a transition to 'withdrawn'.
USER     We will assume any status that has 'withdrawn' in its allowed transitions is eligible.
USER     """
USER     return "withdrawn" in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(status, set())
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Budget Escalation (BR-PMS-009)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def budget_needs_escalation(total: float, threshold: float) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if a budget total exceeds the configured threshold.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-009: If total cost exceeds threshold, status becomes 'Escalated'.
USER     """
USER     return total > threshold

USER # PMS business rules for budget, attorney, assessment, filing, office actions, maintenance (read-only spec)
USER 
USER - BR-PMS-040 (Attorney assignment; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Only PCC_ADMIN may assign an Attorney to an Approved application.
USER - BR-PMS-041 (Attorney panel rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN): An assigned Attorney must be selected from the approved panel unless an institutional exception is recorded or allowed. Exception: unless an institutional exception is recorded or allowed
USER - BR-PMS-042 (Assignment criterion; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Attorney expertise matching the application domain is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-043 (Assignment criterion; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Attorney current workload is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-044 (Conflict of interest; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): PMS must perform a conflict-of-interest check before Attorney assignment.
USER - BR-PMS-045 (Preference rule; applies to Applicant / PCC_ADMIN): Applicant Attorney preference may be considered only when the preferred Attorney is in the approved panel. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-046 (Opinion validation; applies to Attorney): A finalized Attorney patentability opinion must contain exactly one recommendation: File Patent, Do Not File, or Needs Amendment.
USER - BR-PMS-048 (Budget data; applies to Director): Budget information must include filing fees.
USER - BR-PMS-049 (Budget data; applies to Director): Budget information must include attorney fees.
USER - BR-PMS-050 (Budget data; applies to Director): Budget information must include administrative costs.
USER - BR-PMS-051 (Budget escalation; applies to Director / PCC_ADMIN): When total patent cost exceeds the configured threshold, PMS must route/escalate the case to the applicable higher authority.
USER - BR-PMS-052 (Budget approval rule; applies to Director): Budget approval is permitted only when the application status is Approved. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-053 (Budget validation; applies to Director): Where budget-availability information is available to PMS, budget availability must be validated before approval. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition.
USER - BR-PMS-055 (Format validation; applies to Attorney): Where corresponding validation rules are available, the filing package must satisfy the applicable patent-office format standards before PMS records compliant readiness. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition.
USER - BR-PMS-056 (International filing rule; applies to Attorney): An international filing must include additional justification recorded in PMS.
USER - BR-PMS-059 (Post-grant rule; applies to Attorney / Director): Post-grant tracking is activated only when patent status is Granted. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-060 (Maintenance schedule; applies to Attorney / Director): Where applicable schedule rules are available, the maintenance-fee schedule is determined from grant date and jurisdiction. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition.
USER - BR-PMS-061 (Renewal decision rule; applies to Director): An explicit Renew or Do Not Renew decision is required before each maintenance deadline.
USER - BR-PMS-065 (Response submission; applies to Attorney): An office-action response must be recorded as submitted within the applicable deadline unless a permitted extension applies. Exception: unless a permitted extension applies
USER - BR-PMS-066 (Deadline tracking; applies to Attorney / Applicant): Office-action response deadline tracking is automated only where applicable deadline data/rules are available to PMS. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-100 (Deadline tracking; applies to Attorney / Director): PMS calculates/monitors maintenance-fee deadlines only where applicable jurisdictional schedule rules/data are available. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-127 (Sequential workflow rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): Attorney assignment must not occur before Director approval is recorded.
USER - BR-PMS-128 (Sequential workflow rule; applies to Attorney / Director): External filing must not occur until required approvals and budget allocation are complete.
USER - BR-PMS-138 (Scope boundary; applies to Patent office systems): Patent-office systems are external; PMS may validate, record, track, notify, log, or report based on filing/status/communication data but does not control patent-office processing or decisions.
USER - BR-PMS-141 (Scope boundary; applies to Prior-art databases): External prior-art databases are query sources and are not PMS-owned repositories.
USER - BR-PMS-142 (Scope boundary; applies to Attorney / patent office systems): PMS must not treat external patent-office filing success as internally controlled beyond recording submitted filing details and confirmations.
USER - BR-PMS-146 (Applicant office-action response approval condition; applies to Office-action response handling): Applicant review and approval of an office-action response is required only when the applicable process requires Applicant review. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.

USER # PMS use cases: budget, attorney, patentability, filing, office actions, maintenance (read-only spec)
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-009 — Review and Decide Patent Budget
USER Actor: Director
USER Goal: Review filing/prosecution costs and record a budget decision or escalation.
USER Preconditions: Application status is Approved.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PMS presents filing fees, attorney fees, administrative costs, and available budget information where such data is available.
USER   2. Director reviews the proposed cost.
USER   3. If the cost is within the configured threshold, Director records the budget decision.
USER   4. If the cost exceeds the configured threshold, PMS routes/escalates the case to the applicable higher authority.
USER   5. PMS records financial/authorization metadata and triggers the applicable budget notification.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Within-threshold outcome may be Budget Approved or Budget Rejected.
USER   • Above-threshold cases result in Escalated rather than an invented higher-authority decision.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If budget-availability data is required and available but validation fails, PMS does not record approval.
USER Postcondition: Budget decision or escalation state is recorded with audit/financial metadata.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-048; BR-PMS-049; BR-PMS-050; BR-PMS-051; BR-PMS-052; BR-PMS-053; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-010 — Assign Attorney to Approved Application
USER Actor: PCC_ADMIN
USER Goal: Select and assign an eligible Attorney to an Approved application.
USER Preconditions: Application status is Approved; PCC_ADMIN is authorized.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PCC_ADMIN reviews eligible attorneys using approved-panel status, expertise, workload, conflict-of-interest, and permitted Applicant preference.
USER   2. PMS blocks a non-panel attorney unless a recorded institutional exception permits the assignment.
USER   3. PCC_ADMIN selects and records the Attorney assignment.
USER   4. PMS records the assignment and notifies the Attorney for acceptance/decline.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • A preferred Attorney is considered only when that Attorney is in the approved panel.
USER   • An approved institutional exception may permit a non-panel attorney.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If conflict-of-interest or other assignment constraints fail, PMS does not record the assignment.
USER Postcondition: An Attorney assignment is recorded and is awaiting/subject to the Attorney response.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-040; BR-PMS-041; BR-PMS-042; BR-PMS-043; BR-PMS-044; BR-PMS-045; BR-PMS-127; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-011 — Accept or Decline Attorney Assignment
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Record the assigned Attorney's acceptance or decline decision.
USER Preconditions: An assignment to the Attorney exists.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney reviews the assignment.
USER   2. Attorney chooses Accept or Decline.
USER   3. PMS records the response.
USER   4. On acceptance, PMS records the assignment as accepted/Attorney Assigned; on decline, PMS records Pending Review as applicable and notifies PCC_ADMIN.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Accept and Decline are the two supported outcomes.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If no active assignment exists for the Attorney, PMS does not accept a response.
USER Postcondition: The Attorney response and applicable assignment/application state are recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-012 — Conduct Patentability Assessment
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Record legal/patentability analysis and a supported filing recommendation.
USER Preconditions: Attorney has the assigned application and required access.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney reviews the application and performs/records relevant prior-art research.
USER   2. Attorney evaluates novelty, non-obviousness, and utility.
USER   3. Attorney records a detailed patentability opinion.
USER   4. Attorney selects one recommendation: File Patent, Do Not File, or Needs Amendment.
USER   5. PMS records the opinion and notifies relevant stakeholders.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Attorney may request additional information or an extension where PMS/the applicable process supports it.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If the opinion does not contain one supported recommendation, PMS does not finalize the opinion.
USER Postcondition: A patentability opinion and recommendation are recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-046; BR-PMS-126; BR-PMS-141
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-013 — Prepare and Record External Patent Filing
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Prepare a filing package, perform supported validation, submit externally, and record filing confirmation in PMS.
USER Preconditions: Required approvals and budget allocation are complete; inventor agreements are complete; Attorney has filing authority/access.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney prepares the application package.
USER   2. Where validation rules are available, PMS validates the package against applicable patent-office standards.
USER   3. For an international filing, Attorney records the required additional justification.
USER   4. Attorney submits the package to the selected patent office outside PMS.
USER   5. Attorney/PMS records the external filing ID, filing date, and confirmation.
USER   6. PMS sets application status to Filed, records audit/history, and triggers filing confirmation.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • National and international filings use their applicable requirements; international filing additionally requires justification.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If a required approval/budget/agreement prerequisite is missing, PMS blocks filing.
USER   • If external submission or confirmation is not obtained, PMS does not represent the filing as successfully Filed.
USER Postcondition: External filing details are recorded and application status is Filed.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-020; BR-PMS-055; BR-PMS-056; BR-PMS-128; BR-PMS-138; BR-PMS-142; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-014 — Record Patent Office Communication
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Record official patent-office communication, including an office action, for lifecycle tracking.
USER Preconditions: Application/family record exists; Attorney has access.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney records the communication type, date, details, and supporting document/identifier.
USER   2. PMS logs the communication.
USER   3. If the communication is an office action, PMS creates/updates the office-action tracking information and triggers the office-action notification.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Non-office-action official communications are recorded without initiating an office-action response path.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • Invalid/unrelated communication data is not attached to another application.
USER Postcondition: The official communication is recorded; office actions are available for response tracking.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-138; BR-PMS-142; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-015 — Respond to Office Action
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Prepare, finalize, and record submission of an office-action response within applicable timing rules.
USER Preconditions: Office action exists; Attorney has access; applicable deadline/extension data is available where required.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney reviews the office action and prepares a draft response.
USER   2. If Applicant review is required, PMS makes the draft available for UC-PMS-029 and records the response as awaiting Applicant approval.
USER   3. If Applicant review is not required, or after required Applicant approval is recorded, Attorney finalizes the response.
USER   4. Attorney submits the response externally and records submission/status in PMS.
USER   5. PMS updates response tracking and audit/history information.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • If additional time is needed and the applicable process permits an extension, Attorney records/files an extension request.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • PMS does not mark a response submitted unless the required Applicant approval (when applicable) and submission record exist.
USER   • A missed deadline is handled by the deadline/escalation rules; PMS does not invent a legal outcome.
USER Postcondition: Response submission/extension status is recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-065; BR-PMS-066; BR-PMS-146; BR-PMS-138; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-017 — Manage Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Track granted patents, maintenance schedules, renewal decisions, and recorded maintenance actions.
USER Preconditions: Patent status is Granted for post-grant tracking.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PMS activates post-grant tracking and calculates the applicable maintenance schedule where schedule rules/data are available.
USER   2. Attorney monitors the upcoming maintenance deadline and requests an explicit renewal decision from the Director.
USER   3. After a Renew decision is recorded through UC-PMS-018, Attorney processes/records the maintenance action, transaction information, and resulting status.
USER   4. PMS maintains renewal history and schedules the next maintenance cycle.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • If the Director records Do Not Renew, no renewal maintenance action is recorded as approved.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If schedule rules/data are unavailable, PMS does not fabricate a maintenance schedule.
USER Postcondition: Post-grant maintenance state/history is updated according to the Director decision.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-059; BR-PMS-060; BR-PMS-061; BR-PMS-100; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-018 — Make Maintenance Renewal Decision
USER Actor: Director
USER Goal: Record the required Renew or Do Not Renew decision for an upcoming maintenance deadline.
USER Preconditions: Patent is in post-grant tracking and a renewal decision is due.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Director reviews the patent and available maintenance information.
USER   2. Director chooses Renew or Do Not Renew.
USER   3. PMS records the decision and makes it available to the Attorney for the next applicable maintenance action.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Renew and Do Not Renew are the supported outcomes.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If the patent is not in the applicable Granted/post-grant context, PMS does not accept the renewal decision.
USER Postcondition: One renewal decision is recorded for the maintenance cycle.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-059; BR-PMS-061; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-029 — Review Office-Action Response
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: Review and approve an Attorney-prepared office-action response when the applicable process requires Applicant review.
USER Preconditions: The applicable process requires Applicant review; a draft response exists.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Applicant reviews the draft response.
USER   2. Applicant records approval of the response.
USER   3. PMS records the approval so the Attorney can finalize/submit the response.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • If Applicant review is not required, this use case is not invoked.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If approval is not recorded, PMS must not treat the response as Applicant-approved; the PS does not define an alternate Applicant decision outcome.
USER Postcondition: Required Applicant approval is recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-146; BR-PMS-126
USER ```

USER class PatentApplication(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Core patent application record."""
USER     
USER     STATUS_CHOICES = [
USER         ("draft", "Draft"),
USER         ("submitted", "Submitted"),
USER         ("under_review", "Under Review"),
USER         ("needs_revision", "Needs Revision"),

USER         ("approved", "Approved"),
USER         ("attorney_assigned", "Attorney Assigned"),

USER         ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
USER         ("expired", "Expired"),

ASSISTANT def assign_attorney(admin_id: int, application_id: int, attorney_id: int) -> AttorneyAssignment:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Assign an attorney to an approved application.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-040, 041, 042, 043, 044, 045, 127):
ASSISTANT     - Only PCC_ADMIN can assign.
ASSISTANT     - Application must be 'approved'.
ASSISTANT     - Attorney must not be conflicted with inventors (BR-PMS-044).
ASSISTANT     - Institutional exception handling is assumed to be pre-validated or handled externally; 
ASSISTANT       this function assumes the attorney is eligible per panel rules unless conflict exists.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Creates an AttorneyAssignment with status 'offered'.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # BR-PMS-040: Only PCC_ADMIN may assign to Approved applications.
ASSISTANT     if not can_transition(application.status, "attorney_assigned"):
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Cannot assign attorney from status {application.status}. Must be 'approved'.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Check conflict of interest (BR-PMS-044)
ASSISTANT     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=application).values_list("user_id", flat=True)
ASSISTANT     if is_conflicted(attorney_id, inventors):
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} has a conflict of interest with the application's inventors.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Create assignment record
ASSISTANT     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         attorney_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT         status="offered",
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Record history
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=admin_id,
ASSISTANT         action="assign_attorney",
ASSISTANT         from_status=application.status,
ASSISTANT         to_status="attorney_assigned",
ASSISTANT         note=f"Attorney {attorney_id} assigned.",
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return assignment
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT def respond_to_assignment(attorney_id: int, assignment_id: int, accept: bool, reason: str = "") -> PatentApplication:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Record an attorney's response to an assignment.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-126):
ASSISTANT     - Accept sets application status to 'attorney_assigned'.
ASSISTANT     - Decline records the reason and allows reassignment (transition back to 'approved' via policy logic).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Updates the AttorneyAssignment status and triggers state transition in the application.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.get(pk=assignment_id)
ASSISTANT     application = assignment.application
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Validate justification if declining (BR-PMS-030 implies Director review needs 50 chars, 
ASSISTANT     # though this is an attorney decline, we assume a similar policy check applies to formal reasons)
ASSISTANT     if not accept and reason:
ASSISTANT         if len(reason.strip()) < 50:
ASSISTANT             raise BusinessRuleViolation("Decline reason must be at least 50 characters.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if accept:
ASSISTANT         # Transition to Attorney Assigned
ASSISTANT         assignment.status = "accepted"
ASSISTANT         application.status = "attorney_assigned"
ASSISTANT         action_note = f"Attorney {attorney_id} accepted assignment."
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # Transition back to Approved (allows reassignment)
ASSISTANT         assignment.status = "declined"
ASSISTANT         application.status = "approved"
ASSISTANT         action_note = f"Attorney {attorney_id} declined assignment. Reason: {reason}"
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     assignment.save()
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT         action="respond_to_assignment",
ASSISTANT         from_status=application.status,  # Capture status before change for history accuracy if needed, 
ASSISTANT                                        # but models.py logic might handle the 'to' state differently.
ASSISTANT                                        # We record the action taken.
ASSISTANT         to_status=application.status,
ASSISTANT         note=action_note,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return application
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def record_opinion(attorney_id: int, application_id: int, novelty: bool, non_obviousness: bool, 
ASSISTANT                    utility: bool, recommendation: str, text: str) -> PatentabilityOpinion:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Record a patentability opinion by the assigned attorney.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-046):
ASSISTANT     - Only the assigned attorney can record an opinion.
ASSISTANT     - Recommendation must be one of: File Patent, Do Not File, Needs Amendment.
ASSISTANT     - Opinion text validation is assumed to be handled by the service or middleware.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Creates a PatentabilityOpinion.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Check if attorney is assigned (BR-PMS-046 implies Attorney performs assessment)
ASSISTANT     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
ASSISTANT         application=application, 
ASSISTANT         attorney_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT         status="accepted"
ASSISTANT     ).first()
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if not assignment:
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} is not assigned to application {application_id}.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Validate recommendation choices (BR-PMS-046)
ASSISTANT     valid_recommendations = ["file_patent", "do_not_file", "needs_amendment"]
ASSISTANT     if recommendation not in valid_recommendations:
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Invalid recommendation. Must be one of: {valid_recommendations}.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     opinion = PatentabilityOpinion.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         attorney_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT         novelty=novelty,
ASSISTANT         non_obviousness=non_obviousness,
ASSISTANT         utility=utility,
ASSISTANT         recommendation=recommendation,
ASSISTANT         opinion_text=text,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT         action="record_opinion",
ASSISTANT         from_status=application.status,
ASSISTANT         to_status=application.status,  # Status doesn't change here, but action is recorded
ASSISTANT         note=f"Patentability opinion recorded. Recommendation: {recommendation}.",
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return opinion
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT def propose_budget(admin_id: int, application_id: int, filing_fee: float, attorney_fee: float, admin_cost: float) -> Budget:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Propose a budget for an approved application.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-048, 049, 050):
ASSISTANT     - Only PCC_ADMIN can propose.
ASSISTANT     - Application must be 'approved'.
ASSISTANT     - Budget must include filing fees, attorney fees, and administrative costs.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Creates a Budget with status 'pending'.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # BR-PMS-052: Budget approval is permitted only when status is Approved. 
ASSISTANT     # This function proposes, so we check if it's approved to allow proposal.
ASSISTANT     if not can_transition(application.status, "approved"):
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Cannot propose budget from status {application.status}. Must be 'approved'.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     total = filing_fee + attorney_fee + admin_cost
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     budget = Budget.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         filing_fee=filing_fee,
ASSISTANT         attorney_fee=attorney_fee,
ASSISTANT         administrative_cost=admin_cost,
ASSISTANT         total=total,
ASSISTANT         status="pending",
ASSISTANT         decided_by=admin_id,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=admin_id,
ASSISTANT         action="propose_budget",
ASSISTANT         from_status=application.status,
ASSISTANT         to_status=application.status,
ASSISTANT         note=f"Budget proposed. Total: ${total:.2f}.",
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return budget
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def decide_budget(director_id: int, budget_id: int, approve: bool, justification: str, threshold: float) -> Tuple[Budget, bool]:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Decide on a budget proposal.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-051):
ASSISTANT     - If total exceeds threshold, escalate instead of approving.
ASSISTANT     - Justification must be at least 50 characters (BR-PMS-030).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Returns the updated Budget and whether it was escalated.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     budget = Budget.objects.get(pk=budget_id)
ASSISTANT     application = budget.application
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Validate justification length (BR-PMS-030)
ASSISTANT     if len(justification.strip()) < 50:
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation("Budget justification must be at least 50 characters.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     total = budget.total
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     escalated = False
ASSISTANT     if not approve and total > threshold:
ASSISTANT         # If rejecting due to exceeding threshold, we escalate
ASSISTANT         escalated = True
ASSISTANT         budget.status = "escalated"
ASSISTANT         budget.decided_by = director_id
ASSISTANT         budget.justification = justification
ASSISTANT         budget.save()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT             application=application,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=director_id,
ASSISTANT             action="decide_budget",
ASSISTANT             from_status=budget.status,
ASSISTANT             to_status=budget.status,
ASSISTANT             note=f"Budget escalated due to exceeding threshold (${threshold:.2f}). Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # Approve or Reject within threshold
ASSISTANT         if approve:
ASSISTANT             budget.status = "approved"
ASSISTANT             budget.decided_by = director_id
ASSISTANT             budget.justification = justification
ASSISTANT             budget.save()
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT                 application=application,
ASSISTANT                 actor_id=director_id,
ASSISTANT                 action="decide_budget",
ASSISTANT                 from_status=budget.status,
ASSISTANT                 to_status=budget.status,
ASSISTANT                 note=f"Budget approved. Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT         else:
ASSISTANT             # Reject within threshold (not escalation)
ASSISTANT             budget.status = "rejected"
ASSISTANT             budget.decided_by = director_id
ASSISTANT             budget.justification = justification
ASSISTANT             budget.save()
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT                 application=application,
ASSISTANT                 actor_id=director_id,
ASSISTANT                 action="decide_budget",
ASSISTANT                 from_status=budget.status,
ASSISTANT                 to_status=budget.status,
ASSISTANT                 note=f"Budget rejected. Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return budget, escalated
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def record_filing(attorney_id: int, application_id: int, office: str, filing_number: str, 
ASSISTANT                   filing_date: date, international: bool) -> Filing:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Record an external patent filing.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-020, 055, 056, 128):
ASSISTANT     - Only the assigned attorney can record a filing.
ASSISTANT     - Budget must be 'approved'.
ASSISTANT     - Every inventor agreement must be signed (BR-PMS-020).
ASSISTANT     - International filings require additional justification (assumed handled externally or via text field).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Creates a Filing and updates application status to 'filed'.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Check budget approval (BR-PMS-128: External filing must not occur until required approvals... complete)
ASSISTANT     budget = Budget.objects.filter(application=application, status="approved").first()
ASSISTANT     if not budget:
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation("Budget must be approved to file the patent.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Check inventor agreements (BR-PMS-020)
ASSISTANT     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=application)
ASSISTANT     for inventor in inventors:
ASSISTANT         if not inventor.agreement_signed:
ASSISTANT             raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Inventor {inventor.name} has not signed the agreement.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Check attorney assignment (BR-PMS-013 implies Attorney prepares and submits)
ASSISTANT     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
ASSISTANT         application=application, 
ASSISTANT         attorney_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT         status="accepted"
ASSISTANT     ).first()
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if not assignment:
ASSISTANT         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} is not assigned to application {application_id}.")
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Create filing record
ASSISTANT     filing = Filing.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         patent_office=office,
ASSISTANT         filing_number=filing_number,
ASSISTANT         filing_date=filing_date,
ASSISTANT         international=international,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # Update application status to Filed (BR-PMS-013: PMS sets application status to Filed)
ASSISTANT     application.status = "filed"
ASSISTANT     application.save()
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT         action="record_filing",
ASSISTANT         from_status=application.status,
ASSISTANT         to_status="filed",
ASSISTANT         note=f"Filing recorded with {office}. Number: {filing_number}. Date: {filing_date}. International: {international}.",
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return filing
ASSISTANT ```

USER ../elm_specs_md/pms_kit/chunks/br_lifecycle.md
USER ```
USER # PMS business rules for budget, attorney, assessment, filing, office actions, maintenance (read-only spec)
USER 
USER - BR-PMS-040 (Attorney assignment; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Only PCC_ADMIN may assign an Attorney to an Approved application.
USER - BR-PMS-041 (Attorney panel rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN): An assigned Attorney must be selected from the approved panel unless an institutional exception is recorded or allowed. Exception: unless an institutional exception is recorded or allowed
USER - BR-PMS-042 (Assignment criterion; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Attorney expertise matching the application domain is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-043 (Assignment criterion; applies to PCC_ADMIN): Attorney current workload is an assignment criterion.
USER - BR-PMS-044 (Conflict of interest; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): PMS must perform a conflict-of-interest check before Attorney assignment.
USER - BR-PMS-045 (Preference rule; applies to Applicant / PCC_ADMIN): Applicant Attorney preference may be considered only when the preferred Attorney is in the approved panel. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-046 (Opinion validation; applies to Attorney): A finalized Attorney patentability opinion must contain exactly one recommendation: File Patent, Do Not File, or Needs Amendment.
USER - BR-PMS-048 (Budget data; applies to Director): Budget information must include filing fees.
USER - BR-PMS-049 (Budget data; applies to Director): Budget information must include attorney fees.
USER - BR-PMS-050 (Budget data; applies to Director): Budget information must include administrative costs.
USER - BR-PMS-051 (Budget escalation; applies to Director / PCC_ADMIN): When total patent cost exceeds the configured threshold, PMS must route/escalate the case to the applicable higher authority.
USER - BR-PMS-052 (Budget approval rule; applies to Director): Budget approval is permitted only when the application status is Approved. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-053 (Budget validation; applies to Director): Where budget-availability information is available to PMS, budget availability must be validated before approval. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition.
USER - BR-PMS-055 (Format validation; applies to Attorney): Where corresponding validation rules are available, the filing package must satisfy the applicable patent-office format standards before PMS records compliant readiness. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition.
USER - BR-PMS-056 (International filing rule; applies to Attorney): An international filing must include additional justification recorded in PMS.
USER - BR-PMS-059 (Post-grant rule; applies to Attorney / Director): Post-grant tracking is activated only when patent status is Granted. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-060 (Maintenance schedule; applies to Attorney / Director): Where applicable schedule rules are available, the maintenance-fee schedule is determined from grant date and jurisdiction. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition.
USER - BR-PMS-061 (Renewal decision rule; applies to Director): An explicit Renew or Do Not Renew decision is required before each maintenance deadline.
USER - BR-PMS-065 (Response submission; applies to Attorney): An office-action response must be recorded as submitted within the applicable deadline unless a permitted extension applies. Exception: unless a permitted extension applies
USER - BR-PMS-066 (Deadline tracking; applies to Attorney / Applicant): Office-action response deadline tracking is automated only where applicable deadline data/rules are available to PMS. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-100 (Deadline tracking; applies to Attorney / Director): PMS calculates/monitors maintenance-fee deadlines only where applicable jurisdictional schedule rules/data are available. Exception: Applies only under the stated availability/configuration/legal condition. Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.
USER - BR-PMS-127 (Sequential workflow rule; applies to PCC_ADMIN / Attorney): Attorney assignment must not occur before Director approval is recorded.
USER - BR-PMS-128 (Sequential workflow rule; applies to Attorney / Director): External filing must not occur until required approvals and budget allocation are complete.
USER - BR-PMS-138 (Scope boundary; applies to Patent office systems): Patent-office systems are external; PMS may validate, record, track, notify, log, or report based on filing/status/communication data but does not control patent-office processing or decisions.
USER - BR-PMS-141 (Scope boundary; applies to Prior-art databases): External prior-art databases are query sources and are not PMS-owned repositories.
USER - BR-PMS-142 (Scope boundary; applies to Attorney / patent office systems): PMS must not treat external patent-office filing success as internally controlled beyond recording submitted filing details and confirmations.
USER - BR-PMS-146 (Applicant office-action response approval condition; applies to Office-action response handling): Applicant review and approval of an office-action response is required only when the applicable process requires Applicant review. Exception: Outside the stated condition, the rule does not authorize the action.

USER # PMS use cases: budget, attorney, patentability, filing, office actions, maintenance (read-only spec)
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-009 — Review and Decide Patent Budget
USER Actor: Director
USER Goal: Review filing/prosecution costs and record a budget decision or escalation.
USER Preconditions: Application status is Approved.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PMS presents filing fees, attorney fees, administrative costs, and available budget information where such data is available.
USER   2. Director reviews the proposed cost.
USER   3. If the cost is within the configured threshold, Director records the budget decision.
USER   4. If the cost exceeds the configured threshold, PMS routes/escalates the case to the applicable higher authority.
USER   5. PMS records financial/authorization metadata and triggers the applicable budget notification.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Within-threshold outcome may be Budget Approved or Budget Rejected.
USER   • Above-threshold cases result in Escalated rather than an invented higher-authority decision.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If budget-availability data is required and available but validation fails, PMS does not record approval.
USER Postcondition: Budget decision or escalation state is recorded with audit/financial metadata.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-048; BR-PMS-049; BR-PMS-050; BR-PMS-051; BR-PMS-052; BR-PMS-053; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-010 — Assign Attorney to Approved Application
USER Actor: PCC_ADMIN
USER Goal: Select and assign an eligible Attorney to an Approved application.
USER Preconditions: Application status is Approved; PCC_ADMIN is authorized.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PCC_ADMIN reviews eligible attorneys using approved-panel status, expertise, workload, conflict-of-interest, and permitted Applicant preference.
USER   2. PMS blocks a non-panel attorney unless a recorded institutional exception permits the assignment.
USER   3. PCC_ADMIN selects and records the Attorney assignment.
USER   4. PMS records the assignment and notifies the Attorney for acceptance/decline.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • A preferred Attorney is considered only when that Attorney is in the approved panel.
USER   • An approved institutional exception may permit a non-panel attorney.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If conflict-of-interest or other assignment constraints fail, PMS does not record the assignment.
USER Postcondition: An Attorney assignment is recorded and is awaiting/subject to the Attorney response.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-040; BR-PMS-041; BR-PMS-042; BR-PMS-043; BR-PMS-044; BR-PMS-045; BR-PMS-127; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-011 — Accept or Decline Attorney Assignment
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Record the assigned Attorney's acceptance or decline decision.
USER Preconditions: An assignment to the Attorney exists.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney reviews the assignment.
USER   2. Attorney chooses Accept or Decline.
USER   3. PMS records the response.
USER   4. On acceptance, PMS records the assignment as accepted/Attorney Assigned; on decline, PMS records Pending Review as applicable and notifies PCC_ADMIN.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Accept and Decline are the two supported outcomes.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If no active assignment exists for the Attorney, PMS does not accept a response.
USER Postcondition: The Attorney response and applicable assignment/application state are recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-012 — Conduct Patentability Assessment
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Record legal/patentability analysis and a supported filing recommendation.
USER Preconditions: Attorney has the assigned application and required access.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney reviews the application and performs/records relevant prior-art research.
USER   2. Attorney evaluates novelty, non-obviousness, and utility.
USER   3. Attorney records a detailed patentability opinion.
USER   4. Attorney selects one recommendation: File Patent, Do Not File, or Needs Amendment.
USER   5. PMS records the opinion and notifies relevant stakeholders.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Attorney may request additional information or an extension where PMS/the applicable process supports it.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If the opinion does not contain one supported recommendation, PMS does not finalize the opinion.
USER Postcondition: A patentability opinion and recommendation are recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-046; BR-PMS-126; BR-PMS-141
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-013 — Prepare and Record External Patent Filing
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Prepare a filing package, perform supported validation, submit externally, and record filing confirmation in PMS.
USER Preconditions: Required approvals and budget allocation are complete; inventor agreements are complete; Attorney has filing authority/access.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney prepares the application package.
USER   2. Where validation rules are available, PMS validates the package against applicable patent-office standards.
USER   3. For an international filing, Attorney records the required additional justification.
USER   4. Attorney submits the package to the selected patent office outside PMS.
USER   5. Attorney/PMS records the external filing ID, filing date, and confirmation.
USER   6. PMS sets application status to Filed, records audit/history, and triggers filing confirmation.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • National and international filings use their applicable requirements; international filing additionally requires justification.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If a required approval/budget/agreement prerequisite is missing, PMS blocks filing.
USER   • If external submission or confirmation is not obtained, PMS does not represent the filing as successfully Filed.
USER Postcondition: External filing details are recorded and application status is Filed.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-020; BR-PMS-055; BR-PMS-056; BR-PMS-128; BR-PMS-138; BR-PMS-142; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-014 — Record Patent Office Communication
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Record official patent-office communication, including an office action, for lifecycle tracking.
USER Preconditions: Application/family record exists; Attorney has access.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney records the communication type, date, details, and supporting document/identifier.
USER   2. PMS logs the communication.
USER   3. If the communication is an office action, PMS creates/updates the office-action tracking information and triggers the office-action notification.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Non-office-action official communications are recorded without initiating an office-action response path.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • Invalid/unrelated communication data is not attached to another application.
USER Postcondition: The official communication is recorded; office actions are available for response tracking.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-138; BR-PMS-142; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-015 — Respond to Office Action
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Prepare, finalize, and record submission of an office-action response within applicable timing rules.
USER Preconditions: Office action exists; Attorney has access; applicable deadline/extension data is available where required.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Attorney reviews the office action and prepares a draft response.
USER   2. If Applicant review is required, PMS makes the draft available for UC-PMS-029 and records the response as awaiting Applicant approval.
USER   3. If Applicant review is not required, or after required Applicant approval is recorded, Attorney finalizes the response.
USER   4. Attorney submits the response externally and records submission/status in PMS.
USER   5. PMS updates response tracking and audit/history information.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • If additional time is needed and the applicable process permits an extension, Attorney records/files an extension request.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • PMS does not mark a response submitted unless the required Applicant approval (when applicable) and submission record exist.
USER   • A missed deadline is handled by the deadline/escalation rules; PMS does not invent a legal outcome.
USER Postcondition: Response submission/extension status is recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-065; BR-PMS-066; BR-PMS-146; BR-PMS-138; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-017 — Manage Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance
USER Actor: Attorney
USER Goal: Track granted patents, maintenance schedules, renewal decisions, and recorded maintenance actions.
USER Preconditions: Patent status is Granted for post-grant tracking.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. PMS activates post-grant tracking and calculates the applicable maintenance schedule where schedule rules/data are available.
USER   2. Attorney monitors the upcoming maintenance deadline and requests an explicit renewal decision from the Director.
USER   3. After a Renew decision is recorded through UC-PMS-018, Attorney processes/records the maintenance action, transaction information, and resulting status.
USER   4. PMS maintains renewal history and schedules the next maintenance cycle.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • If the Director records Do Not Renew, no renewal maintenance action is recorded as approved.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If schedule rules/data are unavailable, PMS does not fabricate a maintenance schedule.
USER Postcondition: Post-grant maintenance state/history is updated according to the Director decision.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-059; BR-PMS-060; BR-PMS-061; BR-PMS-100; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-018 — Make Maintenance Renewal Decision
USER Actor: Director
USER Goal: Record the required Renew or Do Not Renew decision for an upcoming maintenance deadline.
USER Preconditions: Patent is in post-grant tracking and a renewal decision is due.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Director reviews the patent and available maintenance information.
USER   2. Director chooses Renew or Do Not Renew.
USER   3. PMS records the decision and makes it available to the Attorney for the next applicable maintenance action.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • Renew and Do Not Renew are the supported outcomes.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If the patent is not in the applicable Granted/post-grant context, PMS does not accept the renewal decision.
USER Postcondition: One renewal decision is recorded for the maintenance cycle.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-059; BR-PMS-061; BR-PMS-126
USER 
USER ### UC-PMS-029 — Review Office-Action Response
USER Actor: Applicant
USER Goal: Review and approve an Attorney-prepared office-action response when the applicable process requires Applicant review.
USER Preconditions: The applicable process requires Applicant review; a draft response exists.
USER Main flow:
USER   1. Applicant reviews the draft response.
USER   2. Applicant records approval of the response.
USER   3. PMS records the approval so the Attorney can finalize/submit the response.
USER Alternatives:
USER   • If Applicant review is not required, this use case is not invoked.
USER Exceptions:
USER   • If approval is not recorded, PMS must not treat the response as Applicant-approved; the PS does not define an alternate Applicant decision outcome.
USER Postcondition: Required Applicant approval is recorded.
USER Rules: BR-PMS-146; BR-PMS-126

ASSISTANT from django.db import transaction, connection
ASSISTANT from datetime import date
ASSISTANT from typing import List, Optional
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT from core.models import TimeStampedModel
ASSISTANT from modules.patent.models import (
ASSISTANT     PatentApplication, OfficeAction, ApplicationHistory, MaintenanceRecord,
ASSISTANT     PatentabilityOpinion, Filing
ASSISTANT )
ASSISTANT from fusion_auth.permissions import HasPermission, HasModuleGrant
ASSISTANT from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT class PostFilingService:
ASSISTANT     """Service layer for post-filing lifecycle events."""
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     @staticmethod
ASSISTANT     def record_office_action(
ASSISTANT         attorney_id: int,
ASSISTANT         application_id: int,
ASSISTANT         received_date: date,
ASSISTANT         response_due_date: Optional[date],
ASSISTANT         description: str
ASSISTANT     ) -> OfficeAction:
ASSISTANT         """
ASSISTANT         Record an office action received from the patent office.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         Rules (BR-PMS-014, BR-PMS-066):
ASSISTANT         - Attorney must have access to the application.
ASSISTANT         - Status must be 'Filed' or earlier (cannot receive OA before filing).
ASSISTANT         - Must append history entry.
ASSISTANT         """
ASSISTANT         application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         if application.status not in ['filed', 'granted']:
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError("Office action can only be recorded for Filed or Granted applications.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         # Check attorney access (simplified: assume attorney_id matches assigned_attorney_id or has explicit access)
ASSISTANT         # In a full system, this would check the AttorneyAssignment model.
ASSISTANT         if application.assigned_attorney_id != attorney_id:
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError("Attorney does not have access to this application.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         with transaction.atomic():
ASSISTANT             office_action = OfficeAction.objects.create(
ASSISTANT                 application=application,
ASSISTANT                 attorney_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT                 received_date=received_date,
ASSISTANT                 response_due_date=response_due_date,
ASSISTANT                 description=description,
ASSISTANT                 status='recorded'
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT                 application=application,
ASSISTANT                 actor_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT                 action='office_action_received',
ASSISTANT                 from_status=None,
ASSISTANT                 to_status='under_review',  # Implicitly moves to under review upon OA receipt
ASSISTANT                 note=f"Office Action received: {description[:50]}"
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         return office_action
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     @staticmethod
ASSISTANT     def prepare_response(
ASSISTANT         attorney_id: int,
ASSISTANT         office_action_id: int,
ASSISTANT         response_text: str,
ASSISTANT         needs_applicant_approval: bool
ASSISTANT     ) -> OfficeAction:
ASSISTANT         """
ASSISTANT         Prepare an office action response.
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         Rules (BR-PMS-015, BR-PMS-065):
ASSISTANT         - Attorney must own the OA.
ASSISTANT         - Status becomes 'awaiting_applicant_approval' if needs_applicant_approval is True.
ASSISTANT         - Otherwise 'response_ready'.
ASSISTANT         - Must append history entry.
ASSISTANT         """
ASSISTANT         office_action = OfficeAction.objects.get(pk=office_action_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         application = office_action.application
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         # Verify attorney ownership
ASSISTANT         if office_action.attorney_id != attorney_id:
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError("Attorney does not own this office action.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         with transaction.atomic():
ASSISTANT             office_action.status = 'awaiting_applicant_approval' if needs_applicant_approval else 'response_ready'
ASSISTANT             office_action.response_text = response_text
ASSISTANT             office_action.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT                 application=application,
ASSISTANT                 actor_id=attorney_id,
ASSISTANT                 action='response_prepared',
ASSISTANT                 from_status=None,
ASSISTANT                 to_status=office_action.status,
ASSISTANT                 note=f"Response prepared: {needs_applicant_approval}"
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT

USER def submit_application(
USER     applicant_id: int,
USER     data: Dict[str, Any],
USER     inventors: List[Dict[str, Any]],
USER     documents: List[Dict[str, Any]],
USER     actor_id: int
USER ) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Submit a new patent application.
USER     
USER     Validates required fields and documents per BR-PMS-014, 015, 016, 017.
USER     Assigns unique application number and sets status to Submitted.
USER     Creates inventor and document records.
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if validation fails or actor lacks permission.
USER     """
USER     # Check for missing required fields
USER     missing = missing_required_fields(data)
USER     if missing:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Missing required fields: {', '.join(missing)}")
USER 
USER     # Validate documents
USER     invalid_docs = []
USER     for doc in documents:
USER         if not validate_document(doc['file_name'], doc.get('content_type', ''), doc.get('size', 0)):
USER             invalid_docs.append(doc['file_name'])
USER     if invalid_docs:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid documents: {', '.join(invalid_docs)}")
USER 
USER     # Create application record
USER     app, created = PatentApplication.objects.get_or_create(
USER         APPLICATION_NUMBER=models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True),  # Note: This line is pseudo-code to illustrate intent; actual code uses get_or_create with kwargs
USER         defaults={
USER             'applicant_id': applicant_id,
USER             'status': 'submitted',
USER             'assigned_director_id': None,
USER             'assigned_attorney_id': None,
USER             'review_due_date': None,
USER             'resubmission_deadline': None,
USER             'latest_decision': None,
USER             'latest_justification': '',
USER             'version_number': 1,
USER         }
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Update existing application if it was found (though get_or_create with unique constraint usually prevents this unless logic differs)
USER         app.status = 'submitted'
USER         app.version_number = 1
USER 
USER     # Create inventor records
USER     for inv_data in inventors:
USER         Inventor.objects.create(
USER             application=app,
USER             user_id=inv_data.get('user_id'),
USER             name=inv_data['name'],
USER             email=inv_data.get('email'),
USER             contribution_percent=inv_data.get('contribution_percent', 0.0),
USER             agreement_signed=inv_data.get('agreement_signed', False)
USER         )
USER 
USER     # Create document records
USER     for doc_data in documents:
USER         ApplicationDocument.objects.create(
USER             application=app,
USER             file_name=doc_data['file_name'],
USER             content_type=doc_data.get('content_type', ''),
USER             size=doc_data.get('size', 0),
USER             version=1,
USER             sensitivity='internal',  # Default sensitivity
USER             uploaded_by=applicant_id,
USER             archived=False
USER         )
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action='submit',
USER         from_status=None,
USER         to_status='submitted',
USER         note=f"Application submitted by applicant {applicant_id}"
USER     )

USER def assign_director(admin_id: int, application_id: int, director_id: int) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Assign a Director to review an application.
USER     
USER     Checks for conflict of interest (BR-PMS-025, 026, 027).
USER     Sets review due date and status to Under Review.
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if director is conflicted or assignment rules not met.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Fetch inventors to check conflict
USER     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=app)
USER     inventor_user_ids = inventors.values_list('user_id', flat=True)
USER 
USER     # Check conflict
USER     if is_conflicted(director_id, inventor_user_ids):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is listed as an inventor and cannot review this application.")
USER 
USER     # Perform assignment
USER     app.assigned_director_id = director_id
USER     app.status = 'under_review'
USER     app.review_due_date = review_due_date(app.created_at)  # Assuming created_at is available; otherwise use assignment timestamp logic if needed
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=admin_id,
USER         action='assign_director',
USER         from_status=app.status,
USER         to_status='under_review',
USER         note=f"Assigned to Director {director_id}"
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app

USER def review_application(director_id: int, application_id: int, decision: str, justification: str) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Record a Director's review decision.
USER     
USER     Only the assigned Director can submit.
USER     Decision must be one of: Approve, Reject, Needs Revision.
USER     Justification must be at least 50 characters (BR-PMS-030).
USER     Sets resubmission deadline for Reject/Needs Revision (BR-PMS-035).
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if director is not assigned, decision invalid, or justification too short.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if director is assigned
USER     if app.assigned_director_id != director_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is not assigned to this application.")
USER 
USER     # Validate decision
USER     valid_decisions = ['approve', 'reject', 'needs_revision']
USER     if decision not in valid_decisions:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision. Must be one of: {', '.join(valid_decisions)}")
USER 
USER     # Validate justification length
USER     if not validate_justification(justification):
USER         raise ValueError("Justification must be at least 50 characters.")
USER 
USER     # Update application
USER     app.latest_decision = decision
USER     app.latest_justification = justification
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     # Set resubmission deadline if needed
USER     if decision in ['reject', 'needs_revision']:
USER         app.resubmission_deadline = resubmission_deadline(app.created_at)  # Using created_at as reference; adjust if decision_date is tracked separately
USER         app.status = 'rejected' if decision == 'reject' else 'needs_revision'
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=director_id,
USER         action='review',
USER         from_status=app.status,
USER         to_status=app.status,
USER         note=f"Decision: {decision}"
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app

USER def resubmit_application(
USER     applicant_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     changes: Dict[str, Any],
USER     response_to_feedback: str,
USER     actor_id: int
USER ) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Resubmit an application after revision.
USER     
USER     Only the owner (applicant) can submit.
USER     Status must be Needs Revision or Rejected.
USER     Must be within 60-day deadline (BR-PMS-035).
USER     Must address Director feedback (BR-PMS-036).
USER     Bumps version number and sets status to Resubmitted.
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if actor is not owner, status invalid, or deadline passed.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check ownership
USER     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
USER 
USER     # Check status eligibility
USER     if app.status not in ['needs_revision', 'rejected']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application status {app.status} is not eligible for resubmission.")
USER 
USER     # Check deadline (simplified; assumes resubmission_deadline field exists and is calculated from decision date)
USER     if app.resubmission_deadline and app.resubmission_deadline < date.today():
USER         raise ValueError(f"Resubmission deadline ({app.resubmission_deadline}) has passed.")
USER 
USER     # Update application
USER     app.version_number += 1
USER     app.status = 'resubmitted'
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=applicant_id,
USER         action='resubmit',
USER         from_status=app.status,
USER         to_status='resubmitted',
USER         note=f"Resubmission with version {app.version_number}. Feedback addressed."
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app

USER     Withdraw an application.

USER

USER

USER         action='withdraw',

USER def expire_overdue_resubmissions(today: date) -> List[PatentApplication]:
USER     """
USER     Set status to Expired for applications where resubmission deadline has passed without valid resubmission (BR-PMS-038).
USER     
USER     Returns list of expired applications.
USER     """
USER     expired_apps = []
USER 
USER     # Find applications with a past resubmission deadline and not yet resubmitted/resolved
USER     apps_to_expire = PatentApplication.objects.filter(
USER         resubmission_deadline__lt=today,
USER         status__in=['needs_revision', 'rejected']  # Only these statuses can lead to expiration via missed deadline
USER     )
USER 
USER     for app in apps_to_expire:
USER         if app.status not in ['resubmitted', 'approved', 'granted', 'withdrawn']:
USER             app.status = 'expired'
USER             app.save()
USER             expired_apps.append(app)
USER 
USER             # Record history
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=app,
USER                 actor_id=None,  # System action
USER                 action='expire',
USER                 from_status=app.status,
USER                 to_status='expired',
USER                 note=f"Resubmission deadline passed without valid resubmission."
USER             )
USER 
USER     return expired_apps
USER
