USER def file_appeal(applicant_id: int, application_id: int, grounds: str) -> Appeal:
USER     """
USER     File an appeal against a rejection.
USER     
USER     Only the owner can file.
USER     Application must be in 'rejected' status (BR-PMS-092).
USER     Must be within allowed period (simplified check here).
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if actor is not owner, application not rejected, or outside appeal period.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check ownership
USER     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
USER 
USER     # Check status eligibility
USER     if app.status != 'rejected':
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application must be in 'rejected' status to file an appeal. Current status: {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Create appeal record
USER     appeal = Appeal.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         grounds=grounds,
USER         status='submitted',
USER         decided_by=None,
USER         justification=''  # Can be populated later if needed
USER     )
USER 
USER     return appeal
USER

USER def decide_appeal(director_id: int, appeal_id: int, outcome: str, justification: str) -> Appeal:
USER     """
USER     Record a Director's decision on an appeal.
USER     
USER     Only the assigned Director can decide (simplified assumption that director is linked to appeal or application).
USER     Outcome must be one of: Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, Modify Decision (BR-PMS-095).
USER     Justification required (simplified check here).
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if outcome invalid or justification missing.
USER     """
USER     appeal = Appeal.objects.get(id=appeal_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if director is the one who decided previously (for simplicity, assume director_id matches application's assigned_director_id)
USER     app = appeal.application
USER     if app.assigned_director_id != director_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is not assigned to this application.")
USER 
USER     # Validate outcome
USER     valid_outcomes = ['uphold_rejection', 'remand_for_amendment', 'modify_decision']
USER     if outcome not in valid_outcomes:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid appeal outcome. Must be one of: {', '.join(valid_outcomes)}")
USER 
USER     # Update appeal
USER     appeal.status = outcome.replace('_', '_')  # Map string to status field value (adjust mapping as needed)
USER     appeal.decided_by = director_id
USER     appeal.justification = justification
USER     appeal.save()
USER 
USER     return appeal

USER from django.db import models
USER from core.db.mixins import TimeStampedModel

USER 
USER class PatentApplication(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Core patent application record."""
USER     
USER     STATUS_CHOICES = [
USER         ("draft", "Draft"),
USER         ("submitted", "Submitted"),
USER         ("under_review", "Under Review"),
USER         ("needs_revision", "Needs Revision"),
USER         ("resubmitted", "Resubmitted"),
USER         ("rejected", "Rejected"),
USER         ("appeal_submitted", "Appeal Submitted"),
USER         ("approved", "Approved"),
USER         ("attorney_assigned", "Attorney Assigned"),
USER         ("filed", "Filed"),
USER         ("granted", "Granted"),
USER         ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
USER         ("expired", "Expired"),
USER     ]
USER 
USER     APPLICATION_NUMBER = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
USER     title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
USER     abstract = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     description = models.TextField()
USER     claims = models.TextField()
USER     technical_domain = models.CharField(max_length=100)
USER     
USER     applicant_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="draft")
USER     assigned_director_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     assigned_attorney_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     review_due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     resubmission_deadline = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     
USER     # Latest decision and justification (stored as text for flexibility)
USER     latest_decision = models.CharField(max_length=50, null=True, blank=True)
USER     latest_justification = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     
USER     version_number = models.IntegerField(default=1)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         ordering = ["-created_at"]
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION_NUMBER} ({self.status})"

USER fusion_auth/permissions.py
USER ```
USER """Two gates on every request, both server-side.
USER 
USER     HasModuleGrant   is this module yours at all?   (coarse — drives the sidebar)
USER     HasPermission    may you do this action?        (fine)
USER 
USER Both must pass. The default is deny: a view that declares nothing gets
USER IsAuthenticatedPrincipal and nothing more, so forgetting to think about
USER authorization fails closed rather than open.
USER """
USER from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
USER 
USER 
USER class IsAuthenticatedPrincipal(BasePermission):
USER     message = "Authentication required."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         return getattr(request, "principal", None) is not None
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasModuleGrant(BasePermission):
USER     module_code = ""
USER     message = "This module is not available to your role."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         return p is not None and p.has_module(self.module_code)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasModuleGrant(module_code: str):                       # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasModuleGrant_" + module_code,
USER                 (_HasModuleGrant,), {"module_code": module_code})
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasPermission(BasePermission):
USER     codes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
USER     require_all = False
USER     message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         if p is None:
USER             return False
USER         if self.require_all:
USER             return all(p.has_permission(c) for c in self.codes)
USER         return p.has_any_permission(*self.codes)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasPermission(*codes: str, require_all: bool = False):  # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasPermission_" + "_".join(codes).replace(".", "_"),
USER                 (_HasPermission,), {"codes": codes, "require_all": require_all})
USER ```

ASSISTANT class SubmitApplicationSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
ASSISTANT     """Input data for submitting a new application."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     applicant_id = serializers.IntegerField(required=True, label=_('Applicant ID'))
ASSISTANT     title = serializers.CharField(max_length=255, required=True, label=_('Title'))
ASSISTANT     abstract = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, label=_('Abstract'))
ASSISTANT     description = serializers.CharField(required=True, label=_('Description'))
ASSISTANT     claims = serializers.CharField(required=True, label=_('Claims'))
ASSISTANT     technical_domain = serializers.CharField(max_length=100, required=True, label=_('Technical Domain'))
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     inventors = serializers.ListChild(
ASSISTANT         child=serializers.DictField(
ASSISTANT             child_keys=['user_id', 'name', 'email', 'contribution_percent', 'agreement_signed'],
ASSISTANT             required=False
ASSISTANT         ),
ASSISTANT         required=True,
ASSISTANT         label=_('Inventors')
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     documents = serializers.ListChild(
ASSISTANT         child=serializers.DictField(
ASSISTANT             child_keys=['file_name', 'content_type', 'size'],
ASSISTANT             required=True
ASSISTANT         ),
ASSISTANT         required=True,
ASSISTANT         label=_('Documents')
ASSISTANT     )

ASSISTANT from rest_framework import generics, mixins, status
ASSISTANT from rest_framework.decorators import action
ASSISTANT from rest_framework.response import Response

ASSISTANT from ..models import (
ASSISTANT     PatentApplication, Inventor, ApplicationDocument,
ASSISTANT     AttorneyAssignment, PatentabilityOpinion, Budget, Filing,

ASSISTANT class PatentApplicationListMixin(generics.ListAPIView):
ASSISTANT     """Base mixin for list views that filter by the requesting principal."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     def get_queryset(self):
ASSISTANT         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
ASSISTANT         if not principal:
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Filter based on user kind/role logic (simplified for this step)
ASSISTANT         # In a real system, this would check roles against registry.py ROLE_GRANTS
ASSISTANT         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id)
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'director':
ASSISTANT             # Directors can review applications assigned to them
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id)
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
ASSISTANT         else:  # Default to self or all if logic is complex, here we assume admin access for safety in this snippet context or extend based on specific role checks
ASSISTANT             # For simplicity in this step, assuming Admin sees all, others see self or assigned
ASSISTANT             # A robust implementation would check registry.py ROLE_GRANTS explicitly
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT # --- Application Views ---
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT class ApplicationListView(PatentApplicationListMixin):
ASSISTANT     """List applications for the current user."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     def get_queryset(self):
ASSISTANT         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
ASSISTANT         if not principal:
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id).order_by('-created_at')
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'director':
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id).order_by('-created_at')
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')
ASSISTANT         else:
ASSISTANT             # Fallback for other roles if not explicitly handled above, default to self or empty
ASSISTANT             # Assuming only Student/Director/Admin exist based on context
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT class ApplicationDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateAPIView):
ASSISTANT     """Retrieve and update application details."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.manage")]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     def get_queryset(self):
ASSISTANT         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
ASSISTANT         if not principal:
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id)
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'director':
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id)
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'admin':
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
ASSISTANT         else:
ASSISTANT             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     def get_object(self):
ASSISTANT         queryset = self.get_queryset()
ASSISTANT         try:
ASSISTANT             return queryset.get(pk=self.kwargs['pk'])
ASSISTANT         except PatentApplication.DoesNotExist:
ASSISTANT             raise NotFoundResponse("Application not found.")

ASSISTANT class SubmitApplicationView(generics.CreateAPIView):
ASSISTANT     """Submit a new patent application."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.create")]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
ASSISTANT         try:
ASSISTANT             data = request.data
ASSISTANT             applicant_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             # Validate and submit via service
ASSISTANT             app = submit_application(
ASSISTANT                 applicant_id=applicant_id,
ASSISTANT                 data=data,
ASSISTANT                 inventors=request.data.get('inventors', []),
ASSISTANT                 documents=request.data.get('documents', []),
ASSISTANT                 actor_id=applicant_id
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             return Response(PatentApplicationSerializer(app).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
ASSISTANT         except ValueError as e:
ASSISTANT             return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT class AssignDirectorView(generics.UpdateAPIView):
ASSISTANT     """Assign a director to an application."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.manage")]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     def put(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
ASSISTANT         try:
ASSISTANT             app = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=kwargs['pk'])
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             data = request.data
ASSISTANT             director_id = data.get('director_id')
ASSISTANT             admin_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             # Validate and assign via service
ASSISTANT             app = assign_director(
ASSISTANT                 admin_id=admin_id,
ASSISTANT                 application_id=app.id,
ASSISTANT                 director_id=director_id
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             return Response(PatentApplicationSerializer(app).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
ASSISTANT         except ValueError as e:
ASSISTANT             return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.manage")]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
ASSISTANT         try:
ASSISTANT             app = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=kwargs['pk'])
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             data = request.data
ASSISTANT             applicant_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             # Validate and file appeal via service
ASSISTANT             appeal = file_appeal(
ASSISTANT                 applicant_id=applicant_id,
ASSISTANT                 application_id=app.id,
ASSISTANT                 grounds=data.get('grounds')
ASSISTANT             )

ASSISTANT         try:
ASSISTANT             appeal = Appeal.objects.get(pk=kwargs['pk'])
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             data = request.data
ASSISTANT             director_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             # Validate and decide via service
ASSISTANT             appeal = decide_appeal(
ASSISTANT                 director_id=director_id,
ASSISTANT                 appeal_id=appeal.id,
ASSISTANT                 outcome=data.get('outcome'),
ASSISTANT                 justification=data.get('justification')
ASSISTANT             )
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT             return Response({'id': appeal.id, 'status': appeal.status}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
ASSISTANT         except ValueError as e:
ASSISTANT             return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

USER modules/patent/services/post_filing.py
USER ```
USER from django.db import transaction, connection
USER from datetime import date
USER from typing import List, Optional
USER 
USER from core.models import TimeStampedModel
USER from modules.patent.models import (
USER     PatentApplication, OfficeAction, ApplicationHistory, MaintenanceRecord,
USER     PatentabilityOpinion, Filing
USER )
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasPermission, HasModuleGrant
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER 
USER 
USER class PostFilingService:
USER     """Service layer for post-filing lifecycle events."""
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def record_office_action(
USER         attorney_id: int,
USER         application_id: int,
USER         received_date: date,
USER         response_due_date: Optional[date],
USER         description: str
USER     ) -> OfficeAction:
USER         """
USER         Record an office action received from the patent office.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-014, BR-PMS-066):
USER         - Attorney must have access to the application.
USER         - Status must be 'Filed' or earlier (cannot receive OA before filing).
USER         - Must append history entry.
USER         """
USER         application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER 
USER         if application.status not in ['filed', 'granted']:
USER             raise ValueError("Office action can only be recorded for Filed or Granted applications.")
USER 
USER         # Check attorney access (simplified: assume attorney_id matches assigned_attorney_id or has explicit access)
USER         # In a full system, this would check the AttorneyAssignment model.
USER         if application.assigned_attorney_id != attorney_id:
USER             raise ValueError("Attorney does not have access to this application.")
USER 
USER         with transaction.atomic():
USER             office_action = OfficeAction.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER                 received_date=received_date,
USER                 response_due_date=response_due_date,
USER                 description=description,
USER                 status='recorded'
USER             )
USER 
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=attorney_id,
USER                 action='office_action_received',
USER                 from_status=None,
USER                 to_status='under_review',  # Implicitly moves to under review upon OA receipt
USER                 note=f"Office Action received: {description[:50]}"
USER             )
USER 
USER         return office_action
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def prepare_response(
USER         attorney_id: int,
USER         office_action_id: int,
USER         response_text: str,
USER         needs_applicant_approval: bool
USER     ) -> OfficeAction:
USER         """
USER         Prepare an office action response.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-015, BR-PMS-065):
USER         - Attorney must own the OA.
USER         - Status becomes 'awaiting_applicant_approval' if needs_applicant_approval is True.
USER         - Otherwise 'response_ready'.
USER         - Must append history entry.
USER         """
USER         office_action = OfficeAction.objects.get(pk=office_action_id)
USER 
USER         application = office_action.application
USER 
USER         # Verify attorney ownership
USER         if office_action.attorney_id != attorney_id:
USER             raise ValueError("Attorney does not own this office action.")
USER 
USER         with transaction.atomic():
USER             office_action.status = 'awaiting_applicant_approval' if needs_applicant_approval else 'response_ready'
USER             office_action.response_text = response_text
USER             office_action.save()
USER 
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=attorney_id,
USER                 action='response_prepared',
USER                 from_status=None,
USER                 to_status=office_action.status,
USER                 note=f"Response prepared: {needs_applicant_approval}"
USER             )
USER 
USER         return office_action
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def approve_response(
USER         applicant_id: int,
USER         office_action_id: int
USER     ) -> OfficeAction:
USER         """
USER         Approve an office action response.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-015, BR-PMS-0146):
USER         - Only the application owner (Applicant) can approve.
USER         - Status must be 'awaiting_applicant_approval'.
USER         - On approval, status becomes 'applicant_approved', allowing Attorney to submit.
USER         - Must append history entry.
USER         """
USER         office_action = OfficeAction.objects.get(pk=office_action_id)
USER 
USER         application = office_action.application
USER 
USER         # Verify applicant ownership (simplified: check if applicant_id matches a known owner or is the logged-in principal's ID)
USER         # Since we don't have a User model, we assume applicant_id corresponds to the entity that owns the application.
USER         # In this context, 'applicant_id' likely refers to the user_id of the applicant IAM system.
USER         if office_action.status != 'awaiting_applicant_approval':
USER             raise ValueError("Response must be awaiting applicant approval.")
USER 
USER         with transaction.atomic():
USER             office_action.status = 'applicant_approved'
USER             office_action.save()
USER 
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=applicant_id,
USER                 action='response_approved',
USER                 from_status=None,
USER                 to_status='applicant_approved',
USER                 note="Applicant approved the response."
USER             )
USER 
USER         return office_action
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def submit_response(
USER         attorney_id: int,
USER         office_action_id: int
USER     ) -> OfficeAction:
USER         """
USER         Submit an office action response.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-015, BR-PMS-065):
USER         - Attorney must own the OA.
USER         - Status must be 'response_ready'.
USER         - Must check if response_due_date has passed (simplified: raise error if past).
USER         - On success, status becomes 'response_submitted'.
USER         - Must append history entry.
USER         """
USER         office_action = OfficeAction.objects.get(pk=office_action_id)
USER 
USER         application = office_action.application
USER 
USER         # Verify attorney ownership
USER         if office_action.attorney_id != attorney_id:
USER             raise ValueError("Attorney does not own this office action.")
USER 
USER         # Check deadline (simplified logic)
USER         if office_action.response_due_date and office_action.response_due_date < date.today():
USER             raise ValueError("Response due date has passed. Cannot submit late response.")
USER 
USER         with transaction.atomic():
USER             office_action.status = 'response_submitted'
USER             office_action.save()
USER 
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=attorney_id,
USER                 action='response_submitted',
USER                 from_status=None,
USER                 to_status='response_submitted',
USER                 note="Response submitted to patent office."
USER             )
USER 
USER         return office_action
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def record_grant(
USER         attorney_id: int,
USER         application_id: int,
USER         grant_number: str,
USER         grant_date: date
USER     ) -> PatentApplication:
USER         """
USER         Record a patent grant.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-059):
USER         - Only for applications that are 'filed' or 'granted'.
USER         - Status must be updated to 'granted'.
USER         - Must append history entry.
USER         """
USER         application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER 
USER         if application.status not in ['filed', 'granted']:
USER             raise ValueError("Grant can only be recorded for Filed or Granted applications.")
USER 
USER         # Verify attorney access (simplified check)
USER         if application.assigned_attorney_id != attorney_id:
USER             raise ValueError("Attorney does not have access to this application.")
USER 
USER         with transaction.atomic():
USER             application.status = 'granted'
USER             application.grant_number = grant_number
USER             application.grant_date = grant_date
USER             application.save()
USER 
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=attorney_id,
USER                 action='patent_granted',
USER                 from_status=None,
USER                 to_status='granted',
USER                 note=f"Patent granted: {grant_number}"
USER             )
USER 
USER         return application
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def schedule_maintenance(
USER         attorney_id: int,
USER         application_id: int,
USER         due_date: date,
USER         fee: float
USER     ) -> MaintenanceRecord:
USER         """
USER         Schedule a maintenance fee payment.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-060):
USER         - Only for 'granted' patents.
USER         - Attorney must have access.
USER         - Must append history entry.
USER         """
USER         application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER 
USER         if application.status != 'granted':
USER             raise ValueError("Maintenance can only be scheduled for Granted patents.")
USER 
USER         # Verify attorney access
USER         if application.assigned_attorney_id != attorney_id:
USER             raise ValueError("Attorney does not have access to this application.")
USER 
USER         with transaction.atomic():
USER             maintenance_record = MaintenanceRecord.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER                 due_date=due_date,
USER                 fee=fee,
USER                 decision='renewal_requested',  # Default to requested until Director decides
USER             )
USER 
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=attorney_id,
USER                 action='maintenance_scheduled',
USER                 from_status=None,
USER                 to_status=None,
USER                 note=f"Maintenance fee scheduled: {due_date} (${fee})"
USER             )
USER 
USER         return maintenance_record
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def decide_renewal(
USER         director_id: int,
USER         record_id: int,
USER         renew: bool,
USER         justification: str
USER     ) -> MaintenanceRecord:
USER         """
USER         Make a renewal decision for a maintenance record.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-061):
USER         - Only Director can decide.
USER         - Decision must be 'renew' or 'do_not_renew'.
USER         - Must append history entry.
USER         """
USER         maintenance_record = MaintenanceRecord.objects.get(pk=record_id)
USER 
USER         application = maintenance_record.application
USER 
USER         # Verify Director access (simplified: assume director_id is the logged-in principal's ID)
USER         if application.assigned_director_id != director_id:
USER             raise ValueError("Only the assigned Director can make renewal decisions.")
USER 
USER         with transaction.atomic():
USER             decision_text = 'renew' if renew else 'do_not_renew'
USER             maintenance_record.decision = decision_text
USER             maintenance_record.decided_by = director_id
USER             maintenance_record.justification = justification
USER             maintenance_record.save()
USER 
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=director_id,
USER                 action='renewal_decided',
USER                 from_status=None,
USER                 to_status=None,
USER                 note=f"Renewal decision: {decision_text}. Justification: {justification}"
USER             )
USER 
USER         return maintenance_record
USER 
USER     @staticmethod
USER     def get_history(application_id: int) -> List[ApplicationHistory]:
USER         """
USER         Retrieve the history of actions for an application.
USER 
USER         Rules (BR-PMS-0138):
USER         - Returns ordered list of history rows.
USER         """
USER         return ApplicationHistory.objects.filter(
USER             application_id=application_id
USER         ).order_by('-created_at')

USER fusion_auth/permissions.py
USER ```
USER """Two gates on every request, both server-side.
USER 
USER     HasModuleGrant   is this module yours at all?   (coarse — drives the sidebar)
USER     HasPermission    may you do this action?        (fine)
USER 
USER Both must pass. The default is deny: a view that declares nothing gets
USER IsAuthenticatedPrincipal and nothing more, so forgetting to think about
USER authorization fails closed rather than open.
USER """
USER from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
USER 
USER 
USER class IsAuthenticatedPrincipal(BasePermission):
USER     message = "Authentication required."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         return getattr(request, "principal", None) is not None
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasModuleGrant(BasePermission):
USER     module_code = ""
USER     message = "This module is not available to your role."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         return p is not None and p.has_module(self.module_code)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasModuleGrant(module_code: str):                       # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasModuleGrant_" + module_code,
USER                 (_HasModuleGrant,), {"module_code": module_code})
USER 
USER 
USER class _HasPermission(BasePermission):
USER     codes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
USER     require_all = False
USER     message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, request, view):
USER         p = getattr(request, "principal", None)
USER         if p is None:
USER             return False
USER         if self.require_all:
USER             return all(p.has_permission(c) for c in self.codes)
USER         return p.has_any_permission(*self.codes)
USER 
USER 
USER def HasPermission(*codes: str, require_all: bool = False):  # noqa: N802
USER     return type("HasPermission_" + "_".join(codes).replace(".", "_"),
USER                 (_HasPermission,), {"codes": codes, "require_all": require_all})
USER ```
USER

USER class PatentApplicationListMixin(generics.ListAPIView):
USER     """Base mixin for list views that filter by the requesting principal."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
USER     
USER     def get_queryset(self):
USER         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
USER         if not principal:
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER         
USER         # Filter based on user kind/role logic (simplified for this step)
USER         # In a real system, this would check roles against registry.py ROLE_GRANTS
USER         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
USER         
USER         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id)
USER         elif user_kind == 'director':
USER             # Directors can review applications assigned to them
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id)
USER         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
USER         else:  # Default to self or all if logic is complex, here we assume admin access for safety in this snippet context or extend based on specific role checks
USER             # For simplicity in this step, assuming Admin sees all, others see self or assigned
USER             # A robust implementation would check registry.py ROLE_GRANTS explicitly
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
USER

USER class ApplicationListView(PatentApplicationListMixin):
USER     """List applications for the current user."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
USER     
USER     def get_queryset(self):
USER         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
USER         if not principal:
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER         
USER         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
USER         
USER         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id).order_by('-created_at')
USER         elif user_kind == 'director':
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id).order_by('-created_at')
USER         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')
USER         else:
USER             # Fallback for other roles if not explicitly handled above, default to self or empty
USER             # Assuming only Student/Director/Admin exist based on context
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER

USER class SubmitApplicationView(generics.CreateAPIView):
USER     """Submit a new patent application."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.create")]
USER     
USER     def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
USER         try:
USER             data = request.data
USER             applicant_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
USER             
USER             # Validate and submit via service
USER             app = submit_application(
USER                 applicant_id=applicant_id,
USER                 data=data,
USER                 inventors=request.data.get('inventors', []),
USER                 documents=request.data.get('documents', []),
USER                 actor_id=applicant_id
USER             )
USER             
USER             return Response(PatentApplicationSerializer(app).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

USER class ExpireOverdueResubmissionsView(generics.GenericAPIView):
USER     """Set status to Expired for applications where resubmission deadline has passed."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.admin.manage")]
USER     
USER     def post(self, request):
USER         today = request.data.get('today')
USER         if not today:
USER             from datetime import date
USER             today = date.today()
USER         
USER         expired_apps = expire_overdue_resubmissions(today)
USER         
USER         return Response({
USER             'count': len(expired_apps),
USER             'expired_applications': [app.APPLICATION_NUMBER for app in expired_apps]
USER         }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

USER modules/patent/services/legal.py
USER ```
USER from django.db import transaction, connection
USER from datetime import date
USER from typing import Tuple
USER 
USER from core.exceptions import BusinessRuleViolation
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasPermission, HasModuleGrant
USER from modules.patent.models import (
USER     PatentApplication, AttorneyAssignment, PatentabilityOpinion,
USER     Budget, Filing, Inventor, ApplicationHistory,
USER )
USER from modules.patent.domain.policy import (
USER     can_transition, is_conflicted, validate_justification,
USER     budget_needs_escalation, missing_required_fields,
USER )
USER 
USER 
USER def assign_attorney(admin_id: int, application_id: int, attorney_id: int) -> AttorneyAssignment:
USER     """
USER     Assign an attorney to an approved application.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-040, 041, 042, 043, 044, 045, 127):
USER     - Only PCC_ADMIN can assign.
USER     - Application must be 'approved'.
USER     - Attorney must not be conflicted with inventors (BR-PMS-044).
USER     - Institutional exception handling is assumed to be pre-validated or handled externally; 
USER       this function assumes the attorney is eligible per panel rules unless conflict exists.
USER     
USER     Creates an AttorneyAssignment with status 'offered'.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # BR-PMS-040: Only PCC_ADMIN may assign to Approved applications.
USER     if not can_transition(application.status, "attorney_assigned"):
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Cannot assign attorney from status {application.status}. Must be 'approved'.")
USER     
USER     # Check conflict of interest (BR-PMS-044)
USER     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=application).values_list("user_id", flat=True)
USER     if is_conflicted(attorney_id, inventors):
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} has a conflict of interest with the application's inventors.")
USER     
USER     # Create assignment record
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         status="offered",
USER     )
USER     
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=admin_id,
USER         action="assign_attorney",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status="attorney_assigned",
USER         note=f"Attorney {attorney_id} assigned.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return assignment
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_assignment(attorney_id: int, assignment_id: int, accept: bool, reason: str = "") -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Record an attorney's response to an assignment.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-126):
USER     - Accept sets application status to 'attorney_assigned'.
USER     - Decline records the reason and allows reassignment (transition back to 'approved' via policy logic).
USER     
USER     Updates the AttorneyAssignment status and triggers state transition in the application.
USER     """
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.get(pk=assignment_id)
USER     application = assignment.application
USER     
USER     # Validate justification if declining (BR-PMS-030 implies Director review needs 50 chars, 
USER     # though this is an attorney decline, we assume a similar policy check applies to formal reasons)
USER     if not accept and reason:
USER         if len(reason.strip()) < 50:
USER             raise BusinessRuleViolation("Decline reason must be at least 50 characters.")
USER     
USER     if accept:
USER         # Transition to Attorney Assigned
USER         assignment.status = "accepted"
USER         application.status = "attorney_assigned"
USER         action_note = f"Attorney {attorney_id} accepted assignment."
USER     else:
USER         # Transition back to Approved (allows reassignment)
USER         assignment.status = "declined"
USER         application.status = "approved"
USER         action_note = f"Attorney {attorney_id} declined assignment. Reason: {reason}"
USER     
USER     assignment.save()
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=attorney_id,
USER         action="respond_to_assignment",
USER         from_status=application.status,  # Capture status before change for history accuracy if needed, 
USER                                        # but models.py logic might handle the 'to' state differently.
USER                                        # We record the action taken.
USER         to_status=application.status,
USER         note=action_note,
USER     )
USER     
USER     return application
USER 
USER 
USER def record_opinion(attorney_id: int, application_id: int, novelty: bool, non_obviousness: bool, 
USER                    utility: bool, recommendation: str, text: str) -> PatentabilityOpinion:
USER     """
USER     Record a patentability opinion by the assigned attorney.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-046):
USER     - Only the assigned attorney can record an opinion.
USER     - Recommendation must be one of: File Patent, Do Not File, Needs Amendment.
USER     - Opinion text validation is assumed to be handled by the service or middleware.
USER     
USER     Creates a PatentabilityOpinion.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # Check if attorney is assigned (BR-PMS-046 implies Attorney performs assessment)
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
USER         application=application, 
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         status="accepted"
USER     ).first()
USER     
USER     if not assignment:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} is not assigned to application {application_id}.")
USER     
USER     # Validate recommendation choices (BR-PMS-046)
USER     valid_recommendations = ["file_patent", "do_not_file", "needs_amendment"]
USER     if recommendation not in valid_recommendations:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Invalid recommendation. Must be one of: {valid_recommendations}.")
USER     
USER     opinion = PatentabilityOpinion.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         novelty=novelty,
USER         non_obviousness=non_obviousness,
USER         utility=utility,
USER         recommendation=recommendation,
USER         opinion_text=text,
USER     )
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=attorney_id,
USER         action="record_opinion",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status=application.status,  # Status doesn't change here, but action is recorded
USER         note=f"Patentability opinion recorded. Recommendation: {recommendation}.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return opinion
USER 
USER 
USER def propose_budget(admin_id: int, application_id: int, filing_fee: float, attorney_fee: float, admin_cost: float) -> Budget:
USER     """
USER     Propose a budget for an approved application.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-048, 049, 050):
USER     - Only PCC_ADMIN can propose.
USER     - Application must be 'approved'.
USER     - Budget must include filing fees, attorney fees, and administrative costs.
USER     
USER     Creates a Budget with status 'pending'.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # BR-PMS-052: Budget approval is permitted only when status is Approved. 
USER     # This function proposes, so we check if it's approved to allow proposal.
USER     if not can_transition(application.status, "approved"):
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Cannot propose budget from status {application.status}. Must be 'approved'.")
USER     
USER     total = filing_fee + attorney_fee + admin_cost
USER     
USER     budget = Budget.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         filing_fee=filing_fee,
USER         attorney_fee=attorney_fee,
USER         administrative_cost=admin_cost,
USER         total=total,
USER         status="pending",
USER         decided_by=admin_id,
USER     )
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=admin_id,
USER         action="propose_budget",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status=application.status,
USER         note=f"Budget proposed. Total: ${total:.2f}.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return budget
USER 
USER 
USER def decide_budget(director_id: int, budget_id: int, approve: bool, justification: str, threshold: float) -> Tuple[Budget, bool]:
USER     """
USER     Decide on a budget proposal.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-051):
USER     - If total exceeds threshold, escalate instead of approving.
USER     - Justification must be at least 50 characters (BR-PMS-030).
USER     
USER     Returns the updated Budget and whether it was escalated.
USER     """
USER     budget = Budget.objects.get(pk=budget_id)
USER     application = budget.application
USER     
USER     # Validate justification length (BR-PMS-030)
USER     if len(justification.strip()) < 50:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation("Budget justification must be at least 50 characters.")
USER     
USER     total = budget.total
USER     
USER     escalated = False
USER     if not approve and total > threshold:
USER         # If rejecting due to exceeding threshold, we escalate
USER         escalated = True
USER         budget.status = "escalated"
USER         budget.decided_by = director_id
USER         budget.justification = justification
USER         budget.save()
USER         
USER         ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=director_id,
USER             action="decide_budget",
USER             from_status=budget.status,
USER             to_status=budget.status,
USER             note=f"Budget escalated due to exceeding threshold (${threshold:.2f}). Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
USER         )
USER     else:
USER         # Approve or Reject within threshold
USER         if approve:
USER             budget.status = "approved"
USER             budget.decided_by = director_id
USER             budget.justification = justification
USER             budget.save()
USER             
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=director_id,
USER                 action="decide_budget",
USER                 from_status=budget.status,
USER                 to_status=budget.status,
USER                 note=f"Budget approved. Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
USER             )
USER         else:
USER             # Reject within threshold (not escalation)
USER             budget.status = "rejected"
USER             budget.decided_by = director_id
USER             budget.justification = justification
USER             budget.save()
USER             
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=director_id,
USER                 action="decide_budget",
USER                 from_status=budget.status,
USER                 to_status=budget.status,
USER                 note=f"Budget rejected. Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
USER             )
USER     
USER     return budget, escalated
USER 
USER 
USER def record_filing(attorney_id: int, application_id: int, office: str, filing_number: str, 
USER                   filing_date: date, international: bool) -> Filing:
USER     """
USER     Record an external patent filing.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-020, 055, 056, 128):
USER     - Only the assigned attorney can record a filing.
USER     - Budget must be 'approved'.
USER     - Every inventor agreement must be signed (BR-PMS-020).
USER     - International filings require additional justification (assumed handled externally or via text field).
USER     
USER     Creates a Filing and updates application status to 'filed'.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # Check budget approval (BR-PMS-128: External filing must not occur until required approvals... complete)
USER     budget = Budget.objects.filter(application=application, status="approved").first()
USER     if not budget:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation("Budget must be approved to file the patent.")
USER     
USER     # Check inventor agreements (BR-PMS-020)
USER     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=application)
USER     for inventor in inventors:
USER         if not inventor.agreement_signed:
USER             raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Inventor {inventor.name} has not signed the agreement.")
USER     
USER     # Check attorney assignment (BR-PMS-013 implies Attorney prepares and submits)
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
USER         application=application, 
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         status="accepted"
USER     ).first()
USER     
USER     if not assignment:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} is not assigned to application {application_id}.")
USER     
USER     # Create filing record
USER     filing = Filing.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         patent_office=office,
USER         filing_number=filing_number,
USER         filing_date=filing_date,
USER         international=international,
USER     )
USER     
USER     # Update application status to Filed (BR-PMS-013: PMS sets application status to Filed)
USER     application.status = "filed"
USER     application.save()
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=attorney_id,
USER         action="record_filing",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status="filed",
USER         note=f"Filing recorded with {office}. Number: {filing_number}. Date: {filing_date}. International: {international}.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return filing
USER ```

ASSISTANT class AttorneyAssignmentListMixin(generics.ListAPIView):
ASSISTANT     """Base mixin for list views that filter by the requesting principal."""
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     def get_queryset(self):
ASSISTANT         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
ASSISTANT         if not principal:
ASSISTANT             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.none()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Filter based on user kind/role logic (simplified for this step)
ASSISTANT         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
ASSISTANT             # Applicants can see assignments for their own applications
ASSISTANT             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
ASSISTANT                 application__applicant_id=principal.user_id
ASSISTANT             ).order_by('-created_at')
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'director':
ASSISTANT             # Directors can see assignments for applications assigned to them
ASSISTANT             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
ASSISTANT                 application__assigned_director_id=principal.user_id
ASSISTANT             ).order_by('-created_at')
ASSISTANT         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
ASSISTANT             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')
ASSISTANT         else:  # Default to self or all if logic is complex, here we assume admin access for safety in this snippet context or extend based on specific role checks
ASSISTANT             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')

USER over HTTP — see fusion_auth/.
USER """
USER import os
USER from pathlib import Path
USER 
USER BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
USER 
USER 
USER def _load_env_file(path):
USER     """Fill in from .env whatever the environment has not already set.
USER 
USER     Local development only in practice: production passes real variables
USER     through systemd, and setdefault means those always win. Without this,
USER     `cp .env.example .env` does nothing and every entry point depends on one
USER     shell's exports.
USER     """
USER     try:
USER         lines = path.read_text().splitlines()
USER     except OSError:
USER         return
USER     for line in lines:
USER         line = line.strip()
USER         if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
USER             continue
USER         name, _, value = line.partition("=")
USER         os.environ.setdefault(name.strip(), value.strip().strip('"').strip("'"))
USER 
USER 
USER _load_env_file(BASE_DIR / ".env")
USER 
USER 
USER def env(name, default=None):
USER     v = os.environ.get(name)
USER     return default if v is None or v.strip() == "" else v.strip()
USER 
USER 
USER def env_bool(name, default=False):
USER     raw = env(name)
USER     return default if raw is None else raw.lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
USER 
USER 
USER def env_int(name, default=None):
USER     raw = env(name)
USER     try:
USER         return int(raw) if raw is not None else default
USER     except ValueError:
USER         return default
USER 
USER 
USER def env_list(name, default=None):
USER     raw = env(name)
USER     if raw is None:
USER         return list(default or [])
USER     return [x.strip() for x in raw.split(",") if x.strip()]
USER 
USER

USER Every *SEARCH/REPLACE block* must use this format:
USER 1. The *FULL* file path alone on a line, verbatim. No bold asterisks, no quotes around it, no escaping of characters, etc.

USER 3. The start of search block: <<<<<<< SEARCH
USER 4. A contiguous chunk of lines to search for in the existing source code
USER 5. The dividing line: =======
USER 6. The lines to replace into the source code
USER 7. The end of the replace block: >>>>>>> REPLACE
USER 8. The closing fence: ```
USER 
USER Use the *FULL* file path, as shown to you by the user.
USER 
USER Every *SEARCH* section must *EXACTLY MATCH* the existing file content, character for character, including all comments, docstrings, etc.
USER If the file contains code or other data wrapped/escaped in json/xml/quotes or other containers, you need to propose edits to the literal contents of the file, including the container markup.
USER 
USER *SEARCH/REPLACE* blocks will *only* replace the first match occurrence.
USER Including multiple unique *SEARCH/REPLACE* blocks if needed.
USER Include enough lines in each SEARCH section to uniquely match each set of lines that need to change.
USER 
USER Keep *SEARCH/REPLACE* blocks concise.
USER Break large *SEARCH/REPLACE* blocks into a series of smaller blocks that each change a small portion of the file.
USER Include just the changing lines, and a few surrounding lines if needed for uniqueness.

USER client/src/lib/http.ts
USER ```
USER /** One axios instance for the whole app, with the interceptors the legacy
USER  *  academic SPA never had. */
USER import axios from "axios";
USER 
USER export const http = axios.create({
USER   baseURL: "/api/v1",
USER   withCredentials: true,          // the session cookie, never a JS-readable token
USER   timeout: 15000,
USER });
USER 
USER /** Paired with the session cookie. Held in memory rather than a readable
USER  *  cookie, which the SPA could not read cross-origin in development. */
USER let csrfToken = "";
USER export function setCsrfToken(value: string) {
USER   csrfToken = value ?? "";
USER }
USER 
USER const UNSAFE = new Set(["post", "put", "patch", "delete"]);
USER 
USER http.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
USER   if (csrfToken && UNSAFE.has((config.method ?? "get").toLowerCase())) {
USER     config.headers.set("X-CSRF-Token", csrfToken);
USER   }
USER   return config;
USER });
USER 
USER let onUnauthorized: (() => void) | null = null;
USER export function setUnauthorizedHandler(fn: () => void) {
USER   onUnauthorized = fn;
USER }
USER 
USER http.interceptors.response.use(
USER   (r) => r,
USER   (error) => {
USER     if (error.response?.status === 401) onUnauthorized?.();
USER     return Promise.reject(error);
USER   },
USER );
USER 
USER /** Pull a usable message out of the platform's error envelope. */
USER export function errorMessage(e: unknown): string {
USER   const env = (e as { response?: { data?: { error?: {
USER     message?: string; details?: { message?: string }[] } } } })
USER     ?.response?.data?.error;
USER   if (!env) return "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
USER   if (env.details?.length) {
USER     return env.details.map((d) => d.message).filter(Boolean).join(" ") ||
USER       env.message || "Request failed.";
USER   }
USER   return env.message ?? "Request failed.";
USER }
USER 
USER /** A `responseType: "blob"` request delivers the *error* body as a Blob too,
USER  *  which hides the envelope from errorMessage. Read it back before rethrowing. */
USER export async function readBlobError(e: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
USER   const res = (e as { response?: { data?: unknown } })?.response;
USER   if (res?.data instanceof Blob) {
USER     try {
USER       res.data = JSON.parse(await res.data.text());
USER     } catch {
USER       res.data = undefined;             // an HTML page, not our envelope
USER     }
USER   }
USER   return e;
USER }
USER 
USER export function errorStatus(e: unknown): number | undefined {
USER   return (e as { response?: { status?: number } })?.response?.status;
USER }
USER 
USER export function requestId(e: unknown): string | undefined {
USER   return (e as { response?: { data?: { error?: { request_id?: string } } } })
USER     ?.response?.data?.error?.request_id;
USER }
USER ```
USER

ASSISTANT  *
ASSISTANT  *  Every entry here has a matching nav item in the server's registry.py, and
ASSISTANT  *  that correspondence matters: the sidebar is built server-side, so a nav item
ASSISTANT  *  without a route here renders a link that goes nowhere. */
ASSISTANT export const routes: RouteObject[] = [
ASSISTANT   { index: true, element: <Navigate to="mine" replace /> },
ASSISTANT   { path: "mine", element: <MyApplicationsPage /> },
ASSISTANT   { path: "submit", element: <SubmitApplicationPage /> },
ASSISTANT   { path: "review", element: <ReviewPage /> },
ASSISTANT   { path: "admin", element: <AdminPage /> },
ASSISTANT   { path: "attorney", element: <AttorneyPage /> },
ASSISTANT ];
ASSISTANT ```
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT client/src/app/registry.ts
ASSISTANT ```typescript
ASSISTANT /** Frontend module registry.
ASSISTANT  *
ASSISTANT  *  `code` MUST match accesscontrol_module.code on the server. An ungranted
ASSISTANT  *  module gets no route at all, so deep-linking it 404s rather than rendering
ASSISTANT  *  blank. `load` must be a STATIC import() literal or Vite cannot split it. */
ASSISTANT import type { RouteObject } from "react-router-dom";
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT export interface ModuleManifest {
ASSISTANT   code: string;
ASSISTANT   basePath: string;
ASSISTANT   load: () => Promise<{ routes: RouteObject[] }>;
ASSISTANT }
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT export const MODULE_REGISTRY: Record<string, ModuleManifest> = {
ASSISTANT   placement_cell: {
ASSISTANT     code: "placement_cell",
ASSISTANT     basePath: "/placement",
ASSISTANT     load: () => import("../modules/placement/routes"),
ASSISTANT   },
ASSISTANT   patent: {
ASSISTANT     code: "patent",
ASSISTANT     basePath: "/patent",
ASSISTANT     load: () => import("../modules/patent/routes"),
ASSISTANT   },
ASSISTANT };
ASSISTANT ```
